
Chu Xian was originally the master of the Holy Land, but he was deposed from his position as the Holy Master and expelled from the sect by a group of elders.

And so, the system arrived!

[The Eternal Strongest Sect System has been activated! Please proceed to the coordinates to create the strongest sect!]

[Congratulations, host! 100,000 disciples of your sect have simultaneously broken through to the next level, granting you 100,000 chances to draw a prize!]

[Congratulations, host! You have achieved the honorary title of the number one sect in the Xuanxiao Continent. You are rewarded with peak Great Emperor cultivation. Please check your account!]

[Congratulations, host! You have led your sect disciples to repel the enemy. All disciples' attack power increased by 1000!]

[Congratulations, host...]

Finally, one day, the Demon Lord looked at the big yellow dog at the entrance of the Lingxiao Sect and grinned wildly!

"Damn! Are you blind? Even the watchdogs are at the Emperor Realm, and you call this a fallen sect?"


Chu Xian, Lord of Yunxiao Holy Land, has lost all his cultivation!

After discussion among the Elders of the Holy Land, it has been decided to remove Chu Xian from his position as Sect Master and expel him from the Holy Land immediately!

He shall never step into the Holy Land again for all eternity!

The position of Sect Master shall be held by Grand Elder Qin Mo!

In the center of the grand hall, the young man in splendid robes had a mocking expression as he looked around, finding that not a single person came forward to speak up for him.

Everyone's expressions were uniformly disgusted and disdainful.

Sect Master, what are you still waiting for? Do you want this old man to personally escort you out?

Sect Master, the Sect has supported you for three years; be content. For the development of the Sect, you should leave now.

Hmph! My Yunxiao Sect is a dignified Holy Land; how can its Sect Master be a cripple? Leave quickly, do not be unappreciative!

...

Chu Xian looked around, then finally gave a carefree smile and nodded.

Three years ago, I had no intention of inheriting my father's position as Sect Master.

It was you all! You insisted on putting me in the position of Sect Master!

Now, my usefulness is gone, so you want to drive me out of the Sect.

Little do you know, the position of Sect Master of the Holy Land that you value so much is like dirt to me, Chu Xian!

Hahaha! Good!

From today onwards! I, Chu Xian, will have absolutely no further connection with the Yunxiao Sect!

With that, he turned and strode away!

Outside the grand hall, all the Disciples of the Yunxiao Sect looked at Chu Xian with a hint of reluctance.

During Chu Xian's tenure as Sect Master, the Cultivation conditions provided to the Sect's Disciples were incredibly generous, so the Disciples within the Sect genuinely revered Chu Xian.

However, Disciples are ultimately just Disciples; not a single person stood up to say a word.

After a quarter of an hour, Chu Xian stepped out of the Sect where he had lived since childhood.

Accompanying him was a young boy and a yellow dog.

Standing outside the Yunxiao Sect's Mountain Gate, Chu Xian turned to look at the plaque above the archway, his thoughts a myriad.

As a Transmigrator, Chu Xian was born in this Yunxiao Holy Land, the number one Sect in the Eastern Wasteland!

His father was the Sect Master of the Yunxiao Sect, and Chu Xian, just like the protagonist of a Transmigration novel, had Cultivation talent that could be described as Heaven-Defying!

Under the nurturing of the Yunxiao Holy Land, Chu Xian effortlessly defeated various Geniuses, solidifying the Yunxiao Sect's reputation as the number one Sect in the Eastern Wasteland!

At that time, everyone only offered Chu Xian flattery, admiration, and submission.

However, three years ago, the Spirit Beast race rioted, and Chu Xian was deeply caught in the beast tide.

His parents sacrificed their lives to repel the beast tide and save Chu Xian.

Chu Xian's Cultivation was also crippled in this battle, and despite trying countless methods, he was still unable to recover.

Because Chu Xian had once apprenticed to a powerful figure in the Central Region of the Xuanxiao Continent, the Elders of the Yunxiao Sect still allowed Chu Xian to become the Lord of the Yunxiao Holy Land.

But a few days ago, someone from the Central Region arrived, and that powerful figure had already removed Chu Xian from his list of Disciples, making Chu Xian's position as Holy Lord a joke.

Haha! Back then, you all insisted that I inherit the position of Holy Lord because you wanted to use the reputation of my cheap Revered Master.

Now that I am useless, I can be discarded at will.

It's truly heartbreaking!

But! My father's Yunxiao Sect, how could I let you all take it so easily?

Yunxiao Sect...

We shall meet again!

As his words fell, Chu Xian turned and left with a carefree air, without the slightest reluctance for the Yunxiao Sect!

The young boy and the dog followed silently behind Chu Xian.

Detected that the Host has no more lingering attachment to the Yunxiao Sect; activating the Strongest Sect System of All Ages.

Please proceed to the designated location to establish a Sect and achieve the Strongest Sect accomplishment.

The Novice Gift Pack has been issued. Do you wish to open it?

Chu Xian's eyebrows raised involuntarily as he looked at the light screen appearing before him!

System!

The Strongest Sect of All Ages!

It came at just the right time!

You drive me out of the Sect, and I'll just establish my own Sect!

What I lost today, I will surely reclaim a hundredfold in the future!

Open Novice Gift Pack!

Novice Gift Pack opened. Congratulations to the Host for obtaining: Great Grade Celestial Body Activation Card X1 (can only be used on the Host's Personal Disciple), Great Grade Celestial Art X1, Sect Construction Card X1, Sect NPC Grand Elder (Emperor Realm, cannot leave the Sect's boundaries).

Note: All items can only be used within the Sect.

Sect Coordinates: Xuanxiao Continent, Eastern Wasteland, Great Xia Empire, East Sea Coast region.

Chu Xian fell into thought as he looked at the items in the System's inventory!

Good heavens!

This System of yours is truly something else!

It didn't give the Host a single benefit! Instead, there are quite a few benefits for Disciples.

Thinking of Personal Disciples, Chu Xian looked at the young boy beside him and said softly:

Jian'er, you could have stayed in the Holy Land.

The young boy clenched his fists and said in a deep voice:

My life was given by Revered Master, and I will only stay by Revered Master's side for this lifetime!

Revered Master, don't worry, I will support you from now on!

Although my Cultivation talent is average, it won't be a problem to feed us two and Big Yellow!

Woof!

Big Yellow, the large yellow dog, barked excitedly from the side.

Chu Xian reached out and rubbed the young boy's head, saying softly:

Jian'er, let's go to Great Xia.

Okay! I'll listen to Revered Master!

The young boy's name was Yu Jian. Three years ago, Chu Xian fell deep into the beast tide precisely because he wanted to save this young boy.

After the beast tide receded, Yu Jian became an apprentice to the crippled Chu Xian, and now he is following Chu Xian away from the Yunxiao Sect.

As for the large yellow dog beside them, it was Yu Jian's pet dog... In the grand hall of the Yunxiao Sect.

The Elders were congratulating the former Grand Elder Qin Mo on his promotion to Sect Master, when an old man suddenly appeared at the entrance of the grand hall.

Qin Mo! You forced the Sect Master away, aren't you afraid of retribution?

Qin Mo, sitting in the high seat, chuckled softly,

Hmph! This Holy Lord not pursuing him to the death was already out of consideration for his father's Face. Do you still want that good-for-nothing to remain in the Sect and waste resources?

I think you're senile!

You!

The old man was about to say more when he saw that the high-ranking members of the Yunxiao Sect in the grand hall had already taken action!

Qin Mo snorted coldly:

Hmph! Throw this old thing into the Cloud Prison and await his punishment!

Pah! Qin old dog! You won't die a good death!

Did you forget when the old Sect Master bestowed upon you the Dao of Cultivation?

One day, the Sect Master will return to take your dog's life...

After the old man was taken away, Qin Mo felt as if something was lodged in his chest.

He ordered: Bring the Thousand Mile Mountain-Viewing Mirror.

Before long, an ancient bronze mirror was brought before him.

Qin Mo injected spiritual power into it, and immediately, the figures of Chu Xian, his Disciple, and a yellow dog appeared in the mirror.

Chu Xian in the bronze mirror seemed to sense something and suddenly turned around!

Chu Xian seemed to have returned to three years ago, to the era when he swept through all the Geniuses of the Eastern Wasteland!

That sharp gaze made Qin Mo's heart tremble!

Crack, crack, crack!

Several cracks suddenly appeared on the bronze mirror!

Not good!

Qin Mo hurriedly withdrew the spiritual power infusion, his heart already in his throat!

That cripple only looked once, why would the Thousand Mile Mountain-Viewing Mirror break?

Could it be that destiny is still with Chu Xian?

But he is already a cripple!

Could it be that all my efforts were in vain?

After a long silence,

Third Brother! Eliminate him in secret!

Yes, Father.

...Revered Master? What's wrong?

Yu Jian noticed Chu Xian's unusual expression and asked with concern.

Nothing much, let's hurry to Yunxiao City and take a dao boat to Great Xia.

That feeling of being spied upon just now made Chu Xian feel uncomfortable all over.

Chu Xian leaped onto the dog, riding Big Yellow directly towards the distant giant city!

Woof?

Ah? Revered Master! Big Yellow is still young, you can't ride him!

Woof woof woof!

You two stop talking nonsense! Isn't it because your Revered Master has no Cultivation? Do you think I want to ride a dog?



2: Arrived at the East China Sea coast! Has Big Yellow become a top-tier combatant?

Five days later.

East Wasteland, Great Xia Empire. A hundred li further east was the East Sea Coast.

Chu Xian looked at the vast empty space before him, and the unease in his heart finally vanished.

"Use Sect Construction Card!"

"Please, Host, name the Sect!"

"Lingxiao!"

"Usage successful! Lingxiao Sect is under construction!"

The next moment!

"Rumble!!!"

A muffled thunderous roar came from deep within the earth's veins. The ground beneath Chu Xian's feet suddenly cracked, and a milky white mist surged from the fissures!

Immediately, mountains abruptly rose, several peaks ascending from the ground, piercing through the clouds!

The Spirit Spring in the back mountain burst through the rock layers with a roar, sparkling under the sunlight!

In just a few breaths, the magnificent architecture of a Sect had appeared before the two people and one dog!

"Holy crap!"

"Ma… Ma… Revered Master…"

Yu Jian stuttered for a long time, unable to complete his sentence.

Big Yellow's dog eyes widened even more, his four dog legs trembling with fear.

"Woof?"

Chu Xian, having seen grander sights, quickly composed himself and explained:

"This was prepared for me by your Grandmaster. There's no need to be surprised."

As soon as they stepped into the Sect, a figure instantly appeared!

"Red Feather of Lingxiao Sect greets Sect Master!"

The woman had fiery red hair like blazing flames, skin as delicate as congealed fat that could be broken with a touch. Her vibrant red attire wrapped around her exquisite body, her figure's curves could be described as perfect.

Her bare jade feet floated silently in the air, unstained by a speck of dust.

The appearance of the woman before him greatly steadied Chu Xian's heart!

This must be the Grand Elder the System provided for the Sect!

An Imperial Rank expert!

However, the System had already stipulated that NPC characters could not leave the Sect's boundaries.

"Grand Elder, you've worked hard guarding the Sect."

Chu Xian said this, but in his heart, he quickly spoke to the System:

"System, will this Grand Elder also kick me out of the Sect one day?"

"Host, rest assured! System products are always top-tier! NPCs are absolutely loyal to the Host!"

"Host has entered Sect territory. System mission activated."

"Main Mission activated: Recruit ten Disciples for the Sect."

"Mission completion: 1/10."

"Mission Reward: Host's Cultivation will be raised to the Peak of the Spirit Spring Realm (incomplete)."

"Side Mission activated: For every Realm Breakthrough by a Sect Disciple, the Host will receive one lottery chance."

"Current lottery chances: 0."

Chu Xian was very clear about the Cultivation Realms of this world, which consisted of four ranks and ten Realms:

Mortal Rank: Qi Condensation, Meridian Connecting, Spirit Spring

Spirit Rank: Profound Core, Divine Sea, Dao Seeking

Saint Rank: Dao Integration, Sub-Saint, Great Saint

Imperial Rank: Great Emperor

Back then, Chu Xian had reached the Hedao Realm!

He was even respectfully called a Minor Saint!

If the Hedao Realm were placed in this Great Xia Empire, it would be an invincible existence!

And the Peak of the Spirit Spring Realm, the mission reward, was already enough to ensure self-preservation in the eastern region of this Great Xia Empire.

After roughly understanding the System's functions, Chu Xian let Red Feather lead them, and the three people and one dog headed towards the Main Peak of Lingxiao Sect.

"Holy crap! Big Yellow, have you ever seen grapes that glow blue?"

"Woof?"

Red Feather explained at the opportune moment: "Ice Flame Crystal Grapes, a Saint medicine. Eating one can make a Cultivator's body produce a wisp of Saint Fire."

Chu Xian's pupils contracted!

Back then, to compete for a wisp of Saint Fire for the Pill Saint of Yunxiao Sect, he had destroyed fifty-eight Sects!

Here, it was just a matter of eating a grape?

That's too ridiculous!

"Hey! Big Yellow! You son of a bitch! That's a Saint medicine! You..."

Yu Jian's curses immediately caught Chu Xian's attention. He saw Big Yellow spew a few wisps of blue flames from his mouth.

The next moment, with a "gulp," he directly swallowed the entire bunch of grapes!

Yu Jian became anxious. He immediately pulled Big Yellow into his arms, his fists raining down on the dog's belly like raindrops!

"Bang bang bang!"

"Big Yellow! You, you, you..."

"This is a Saint medicine! You're just a common dog, you're going to explode!"

"Spit it out, quick!"

"Boom!"

Big Yellow's dog body suddenly erupted with a large amount of blue flames!

Fortunately, Chu Xian was quick-witted and pulled Yu Jian back, otherwise he would have been engulfed by the blue flames!

Chu Xian rubbed his forehead and waved his hand helplessly at Red Feather.

Red Feather immediately understood.

Red Feather lightly raised her jade hand, and a ray of light entered Big Yellow's body.

Then, the three of them saw a big yellow dog Rebirth in blue flames!

A yellow dog with blue flames, although the colors were very abrupt, the Aura that Big Yellow exuded was incredibly fierce!

"Woof!!!"

A dog bark directly forced Chu Xian and Yu Jian to retreat repeatedly!

"What are you barking at?"

Yu Jian, as a fifth-level Qi Condensation Cultivator, was actually suppressed by Big Yellow and unable to resist, only able to roar angrily!

"I said, what the hell are you barking at!"

Saying that, he picked up a stone from the ground and threw it at Big Yellow!

"Thud!"

"Woof woof woof woof~~~"

Big Yellow finally recognized reality and trotted to the side with his tail between his legs.

Chu Xian saw more than Yu Jian.

"Big Yellow actually developed Cultivation! It seems to be around the fifth level of Meridian Connecting!"

"A bunch of Saint medicine actually allowed an ordinary common dog to leap into a Spirit Beast!"

"And it even directly skipped Qi Condensation, becoming a Meridian Connecting Realm Spirit Beast!"

"This is getting more and more outrageous!"

Immediately, Chu Xian's gaze towards Red Feather was filled with endless security!

Is this an Imperial Rank expert? Effortlessly changing the fate of a creature!

Now it was good. Aside from Red Feather, the NPC, Big Yellow had become the top combat power of Lingxiao Sect!

As the group continued deeper into the Sect, the surrounding spiritual energy became increasingly rich.

"Revered Master... how do I feel that the spiritual energy here is even richer than in our Yunxiao Holy Land?"

Due to the loss of his Cultivation, Chu Xian had no sensation of spiritual energy, but Yu Jian was the first to notice something was amiss.

"Revered Master! I'm about to Breakthrough!"

With that, Yu Jian immediately sat cross-legged and began to focus on his Breakthrough.

In a few breaths, a muffled sound was heard from within Yu Jian's body.

Yu Jian gently stood up, exhaled a mouthful of turbid air, and couldn't help but beam with joy, "Revered Master! I'm at the sixth level of Qi Condensation!"

Chu Xian smiled and nodded, "Hmm! Not bad!"

In the System panel, Chu Xian's lottery chances had quietly increased by 1.

The three people and one dog finally arrived at the Main Peak of Lingxiao Sect.

Chu Xian took advantage of Red Feather leading Yu Jian to choose a residence to open his System backpack.

"Items: Great Grade Celestial Body Activation Card X1, Great Grade Celestial Art X1."

"Use Great Grade Celestial Body Activation Card!"

"Select target: Yu Jian (currently the only available one)."

"Confirm use of Great Grade Celestial Body Activation Card on Yu Jian?"

Chu Xian didn't hesitate at all: "Confirm!"

"Great Grade Celestial Body is activating..."

Yu Jian, who was following behind Red Feather, glowed with golden light, his robes fluttering wildly.

The next moment, his body burst into brilliant light, runes flowed, as if merging with heaven and earth!

The dazzling golden light seemed to tear open the firmament, and every spiritual energy fluctuation made the air tremble!

Yu Jian was like a phoenix Rebirth from fire.

"Ah!!!"

The agonizing pain made Yu Jian scream out.

The excruciating pain throughout his body, as if being cut by knives, immediately made Yu Jian faint!



3: The murderer was Chu Xian!

The golden light emanating from Yu Jian's body shot straight into the sky, a sight that instantly terrified the people along the entire East Sea Coast!

"Ah? Are the sea beasts going to riot?"

"Wuwuwu~ Mom, save me, the sea beasts are coming again..."

Amidst the panic of the Mortals, a black-clothed figure appeared incredibly shocked!

"This aura... why is it so similar to that good-for-nothing Yu Jian?"

"The good-for-nothing Revered Master's good-for-nothing Disciple, did they encounter some opportunity?"

As he spoke, the black-clothed figure was already heading towards Lingxiao Sect.

Before long, he appeared outside Lingxiao Sect.

The man looked at the three characters "Lingxiao Sect" above his head and couldn't help but sneer.

"Lingxiao? What an arrogant claim! Do you want to lord over my Yunxiao Holy Land?"

"Chu Xian, that bastard, truly deserves to die!"

"Today, I shall help my father eliminate him!"

As soon as the man appeared at the entrance of Lingxiao Sect, Red Feather already sensed him.

He transmitted a message: "Sect Master, someone is at the door."

A spiritual energy converged into an image before Chu Xian, and when Chu Xian saw who it was, he raised an eyebrow and said,

"Qin Shuyang?"

"Haha! It seems Qin Mo sent him to kill me."

"Bring him over!"

Qin Shuyang at the entrance was cursing loudly when he suddenly felt a dizzy spell, and the next second he appeared on the Main Peak of Lingxiao Sect.

Chu Xian stood with his hands behind his back, quietly looking at the person before him.

After Qin Shuyang's body stabilized, he also noticed Chu Xian.

Feeling that Chu Xian was still a Mortal, Qin Shuyang immediately relaxed.

"Hehe, good-for-nothing Sect Master, how have you been?"

"You ran quite fast, didn't you? This Saint chased you all the way from the Holy Land to this godforsaken place."

"Chu Xian, when did you become such a coward?"

Qin Shuyang was Qin Mo's youngest son, the third in line.

Because Chu Xian's father had been developing the Sect, Chu Xian was born later, and Qin Shuyang and Chu Xian were Cultivators of the same generation.

It could be said that Qin Shuyang had always lived in Chu Xian's shadow.

Now that Chu Xian was a cripple, Qin Shuyang's mission was to kill Chu Xian, so he naturally wanted to humiliate him thoroughly.

Chu Xian did not answer, merely watching Qin Shuyang show off.

Someone of Qin Shuyang's caliber, three years ago, he wouldn't even spare him a glance!

Now he was swaggering in front of him, showing off?

"Hehe, even if you hide to the ends of the earth, it's as easy as flipping my hand for this Saint to kill you!"

As he spoke, Qin Shuyang already began to circulate his spiritual energy, and his Divine Sea Realm cultivation caused the sand and stones on the Main Peak to move without wind.

Chu Xian finally spoke: "I advise you not to make a move, you will die a very miserable death."

"Hahahaha, you, a cripple, what can you do to me, a Divine Sea Realm Cultivator?"

As he spoke, he slowly walked towards Chu Xian, his Divine Sea Realm aura gradually enveloping Chu Xian.

If Qin Shuyang wanted to kill Chu Xian, he only needed to lift a finger, but he clearly didn't want Chu Xian to die just like that.

Chu Xian endured with his Mortal body, bearing immense pressure.

Sensing the tense atmosphere, Big Yellow galloped from afar.

"Woof woof woof!!!"

Big Yellow leaped up, opened his mouth, and unleashed a spiritual energy attack towards Qin Shuyang.

"Hmm? This mutt has spiritual energy? Interesting!"

Qin Shuyang lightly extended his palm, and Big Yellow let out an "Ow!" as his dog body flew uncontrollably towards Qin Shuyang.

"Enough! Red Feather, cripple his cultivation and throw him back to Yunxiao Sect!"

"I was waiting for that very sentence from the Sect Master!"

No sooner had the words fallen than blood mist erupted from various parts of Qin Shuyang's body!

"Puff puff puff puff!"

Qin Shuyang lay on the ground like a broken sack, blood gurgling out of various Acupoints on his body!

There was no longer any spiritual energy fluctuation within him.

"You... who exactly are you?"

"What kind of demonic art is this?"

"Impossible! I will never become a cripple! Impossible!"

Red Feather walked over slowly, "Sect Master, should I throw him back to Yunxiao Sect?"

Upon seeing Red Feather, Qin Shuyang instantly became terrified!

"How can there be a Saint-rank Expert here?"

Red Feather turned her head and smiled at Qin Shuyang, her voice like heavenly music:

"Saint-rank? Hehe~"

"Those below Saint-rank see me as a frog at the bottom of a well looking up at Bright Moon."

"Those of Saint-rank see me..."

"They will know themselves to be like a mayfly gazing at the azure sky."

When these words came out, Qin Shuyang was initially a little confused, but after carefully pondering them, his eyes suddenly widened!

A Saint-rank compared to this woman is like a mayfly to the azure sky?

Doesn't that mean this woman has already surpassed the Saint-rank?

What is beyond Saint-rank?

Imperial Rank!!!

An Emperor who hadn't appeared on Xuanxiao Continent for countless years!

And she calls Chu Xian Sect Master?

Qin Shuyang was completely dumbfounded!

How could this be?

An Emperor calling a cripple Sect Master?

While Qin Shuyang was in a daze, Chu Xian came before him.

With Red Feather's help, he carved a sentence onto a stone and placed it in Qin Shuyang's embrace.

"Alright, now he can be thrown back to Yunxiao Sect."

"As you command."

Red Feather gently raised her jade hand and suddenly swung it out!

Qin Shuyang's body transformed into a streak of light and instantly flew out!

With a "whoosh," he vanished without a trace; he was truly thrown back!

...Yunxiao Holy Land.

The Sect Protection Barrier flickered for a moment, as if something had suddenly rushed into the Holy Land.

"Thud!"

A corpse heavily slammed onto the Sect's plaza of Yunxiao Holy Land.

The Disciples gathered around curiously to observe.

"Hiss! This is... the Third Saint?"

"The Third Saint... is dead?"

Now Qin Mo was the Lord of the Holy Land, and his three sons had also become Saints of the Holy Land.

The news of Qin Shuyang's death instantly swept through the entire Yunxiao Holy Land.

"The Third Saint is dead! He died a tragic death, his cultivation crippled before he was killed!"

"Alas, the Holy Lord's son died just after he took office; his future days won't be easy."

"Who is the murderer? How dare they so openly throw the body onto the plaza!"

Amidst the discussions of the Disciples, Qin Mo had already appeared on the plaza.

"Third child..."

Qin Mo murmured, walking disconsolately towards the corpse in the center of the plaza, like a walking corpse.

Qin Shuyang's talent could be considered top-tier in this era.

However, he was of the same generation as Chu Xian, and any achievement he made would always be completed later than Chu Xian's, so he had always lived in Chu Xian's shadow.

Qin Mo naturally knew this, which was why he sent Qin Shuyang to deal with Chu Xian, but he never expected that what returned was Qin Shuyang's corpse!

Looking at his youngest son in such a state, Qin Mo felt utterly despondent.

"Shuyang... Father is here..."

As he held Qin Shuyang's corpse in his arms, he suddenly felt something hard pressing against his chest.

Qin Mo trembled as he took out a stone from his son's embrace.

There were only six large characters on the stone!

"The killer is Chu Xian!"

"Bang!"

The stone in Qin Mo's hand instantly turned to dust.

"Chu Xian!"

"I will surely eat your flesh and sleep on your skin!"



4: Immortal Body Awakening! Reward: 10 consecutive draws!

Lingxiao Sect Main Peak.

"I'm going! What is that terrible smell! Did someone poop their pants?!"

Yu Jian suddenly woke up, a putrid stench assailing his senses!

When he saw himself covered from head to toe in a thick layer of black mud, Yu Jian cried!

"I... pooped my pants?"

"I, a seven-foot man, actually..."

Then, Yu Jian transformed into a black bolt of lightning and shot towards the Spirit Spring in the back mountain.

Chu Xian, lying on a stone chair, only felt something flash past his eyes.

"What just went by?"

Elder Chi Yu, while pouring tea for Chu Xian, waved her hand lightly to disperse the foul odor in the air, saying:

"I don't know, it looked like a big black rat, perhaps?"

Big Yellow, by their side, kept rubbing his nose with his front paws.

Finally, Big Yellow lay on his back, all four paws in the air,

"Ugh~~~"

Big Yellow couldn't hold it in after all, and he vomited!

Dogs inherently have a keen sense of smell, and now Big Yellow was a Spirit Beast in the Meridian Connecting Realm, his sense of smell was incomparable to before. The scent on Yu Jian was magnified countless times in front of Big Yellow... For Big Yellow, it was absolute torture!

Upon reaching the Spirit Spring, Yu Jian plunged into the water with a "plop" and began to wash off the grime on his body.

Where the black mud was washed away, his skin faintly glowed with an unusual luster!

"That's strange, I felt so much pain just now, and when I woke up, all this dirt appeared on me..."

Just as Yu Jian was muttering to himself.

"Dong!"

A muffled sound came from within his body!

"Hm? What was that sound?"

Before Yu Jian could react, there were two more sounds!

"Dong! Dong!"

"Ah? What's going on? Is there something dirty in this Spirit Spring?"

"Dong!"

Another sound came out, and Yu Jian finally reacted!

"Bre... Breakthrough?"

"Qi Condensation Peak!"

"Con... consecutively broke through four Realms! When did I become so awesome?"

At this moment, Chu Xian's voice rang in Yu Jian's ears.

"Focus! Sit still!"

Hearing Chu Xian's words, Yu Jian quickly composed himself, sat cross-legged, and began to sort out the massive amount of Spiritual Qi that had suddenly increased within his body.

Yu Jian only felt the Spiritual Qi inside him growing more and more, and his Meridians were already being severely stretched by the Spiritual Qi!

Yu Jian panicked, his eyes tightly shut, and he gritted his teeth, saying: "Revered Master... I won't explode and die, will I!"

Chu Xian's voice was transmitted to Yu Jian again through Red Feather:

"Don't panic! Control the Spiritual Qi within your body, this is a critical moment for Meridian Connecting!"

Yu Jian had heard Chu Xian's lectures before; the Meridian Connecting Realm required a Cultivator to clear and widen the Meridians within their body.

After receiving Chu Xian's reply, Yu Jian's confidence greatly increased.

Before long, an even louder sound emanated from within Yu Jian's body!

"Boom!"

This also meant that Yu Jian had now officially entered the second mortal Realm, Meridian Connecting!

"Continue to stabilize your cultivation." Chu Xian's voice rang out again.

Yu Jian had no choice but to comply.

However, an unexpected event occurred!

"Dong!"

"Dong!"

The more Yu Jian tried to stabilize his cultivation, the faster the Spiritual Qi within him grew!

"Dong! Dong! Dong!"

In the blink of an eye, Yu Jian had already reached Meridian Connecting Fifth Rank!

"Revered Master!!"

Yu Jian wasn't carried away by the sudden surge in his cultivation, because Chu Xian had once said that explosive growth would lead to an unstable Foundation.

Red Feather gently raised her jade hand, and a glow enveloped Yu Jian, sending him back to Chu Xian's side.

His cultivation finally stopped at Meridian Connecting Fifth Rank.

"Revered Master... This is..."

Only then did Yu Jian realize with shock that the surrounding Spiritual Qi was automatically drilling into his body through his pores!

He hadn't even guided the Qi into his body! Why was this Spiritual Qi drilling in by itself?

Chu Xian got up from the stone chair, pinched Yu Jian's shoulder, and praised:

"What a Great Grade Celestial Body, breaking through ten Realms is as easy as drinking water!"

Yu Jian: "Hm?"

"Wait, Revered Master! Celestial Body? Who?"

Chu Xian smiled and raised a hand to point.

"Me? Celestial Body?"

"Hiccup!"

Due to being too bewildered and excited, Yu Jian directly showed Chu Xian what it meant to pass out and start twitching!

Chu Xian shook his head with a smile: "This young man, his sleep quality is quite good!"

He had Red Feather wake Yu Jian, then took out the "Great Grade Celestial Art" from the System backpack and threw it to Yu Jian.

"You are inherently a Great Grade Celestial Body. Now that you have Awakened, cultivating with this set of Immortal Arts will allow your cultivation to advance a thousand miles a day."

Yu Jian received the "Great Grade Celestial Art" with trembling hands, unable to recover for a long time!

The leap from trash to Genius was simply too great!

Three years ago, Yu Jian became Chu Xian's disciple, and in three years, he only barely managed to Cultivate to Qi Condensation Fourth Rank.

Today, upon arriving at the new Sect, he first broke through one Rank due to the rich Spiritual Qi, then immediately Awakened his Immortal Body, directly crossing a major Realm to break through to Meridian Connecting Fifth Rank!

The height Yu Jian originally needed twenty or even fifty years to reach, he now achieved in less than an hour!

Directly saving decades of detours!

Who wouldn't twitch at that?

After sending Yu Jian away, Chu Xian turned his gaze to Red Feather at his side.

"Elder Chi Yu, I have some questions I'd like to ask you."

Red Feather's sweet smile combined with her peerless beauty made Chu Xian even more confused for a moment.

"Sect Master, please ask."

Chu Xian took a deep breath and asked:

"Are you a robot created by the System?"

Red Feather was visibly startled!

"System? Robot?"

"What do I have to do with them?"

Seeing that Red Feather's expression didn't seem feigned, Chu Xian grew even more curious.

"Then are you of the Human Race?"

"Of course I am Human Race, Sect Master. You haven't damaged your Divine Soul, have you? Do you want me to take a look?"

As she spoke, Red Feather was about to reach out to Chu Xian.

"No need, This Sect Master is fine."

He thought to himself: Since Red Feather says she is of the Human Race, she must be able to Cultivate.

Chu Xian asked again: "Then do you know what the consequences would be if you left the Sect's boundaries?"

Because Chu Xian had seen on the system screen that NPCs could not leave the Sect, he raised this question.

Red Feather answered without hesitation: "I would die!"

Upon receiving the answer, Chu Xian's pupils instantly contracted!

Red Feather was an Emperor Realm expert, but she would die if she left the Sect!

Who could kill an Emperor?

After a long silence, Chu Xian spoke:

"Why?"

Red Feather took a small sip of tea and mused:

"It should be similar to some kind of contract, existing deep within my Divine Soul, which I can perceive."

"But where this contract came from, I do not know."

Chu Xian nodded. It seemed that the contract was the System's restriction on NPCs.

He then asked a whole host of other questions, but didn't get any useful answers!

"Forget it, I won't dwell on this anymore."

Chu Xian finally turned his gaze to the lottery function on the System interface!

【Sect Grand Lottery】

【Number of draws: 10 times】

【Note: The first ten consecutive draws guarantee an Immortal Rank item!】

"Hm? Immortal Rank item?"

In Chu Xian's original understanding, there were four Ranks in the world: Mortal, Spirit, Saint, and Emperor. But after acquiring the System, Chu Xian learned of another Rank, and that was Immortal!

It must be an existence even more Advanced than the Emperor Rank!

"This System is reliable! It really gives good stuff!"

"Draw! Draw hard for me!"



5: Take on apprentices! Take them all!

【Ten consecutive draws in progress!】

The system screen before Chu Xian's eyes began to flash rapidly, accompanied by the crisp "ding ding ding" sound of a spinning wheel.

The next second, a golden light illuminated the entire system screen!

【Wow! Golden Legend!】

【Congratulations, Host, you have received an Immortal Rank reward, the bloodline immortal pool!】

【The bloodline immortal pool has manifested in the Sect! Sect Disciples can enter the Immortal Pool to Cultivate, and their Bloodline Power, Physique, talent, etc., will be improved!】

【Note: As long as you soak in the Immortal Pool every day, even a dog can swallow the sun!】

Chu Xian's eyelids twitched as he glanced at Big Yellow beside him.

"Good heavens! Why do I feel like this System was set up for Big Yellow?"

"Big Yellow! I'm taking you to a fun place."

With that, before Big Yellow could react, Chu Xian directly grabbed Big Yellow's head and ran towards the newly manifested bloodline immortal pool!

"Awooo awooo~~"

The pool was simply too terrifying, it looked just like blood!

Chu Xian couldn't help but wonder,

"Is this a proper System? It's not going to make me establish a Demon Cultivator Sect, is it?"

Big Yellow: I'm not human, but you're a real dog!

Immediately after, the dog Bloodline within Big Yellow's body began to change, and specks of golden light started to appear in his blood!

"Whoosh!"

The Qi on Big Yellow's body suddenly soared!

Although his Realm hadn't changed, Chu Xian could clearly feel that Big Yellow looked somewhat majestic!

And he seemed to have become smarter too!

"Hmm? Good dog!"

He pulled Big Yellow out of the blood pool, then Chu Xian leaped into the air and flew directly into the blood pool!

However, Chu Xian stayed in the blood pool for several minutes, but there was no change at all!

system screen:

【Rewards have been automatically stored in various Sect facilities.】

Looking at the constantly flashing screen before him, Chu Xian was on the verge of tears!

"So this is how we're playing, huh? Not a single thing I can use!"

"Take Disciples! I must vigorously take Disciples!"

No sooner said than done, Chu Xian immediately packed his bags and led Big Yellow out of the Lingxiao Sect.

As for Yu Jian's Cultivation, Chu Xian didn't need to worry about it at all; with an immortal body and immortal techniques, he wouldn't encounter any Bottleneck.

What's more, with Emperor Red Feather overseeing things, he could rest assured and go out to recruit Disciples.

Chu Xian took out two Spirit Beast pellets and threw them to Big Yellow, and the scent that could charm countless native dogs instantly made Big Yellow's dog eyes gleam!

"Woof woof!"

Stroking Big Yellow's fur, Chu Xian sighed:

"Alas, to think that I, once the number one Genius of the Eastern Wilderness, now need a native dog to escort me when I go out!"

One person and one dog, gradually moving further away... East Sea Coast, Biluo Town, Tang Family.

"Tang Family clansman Tang Liu, for secretly learning the Family's secret technique, is to be thrown into the death prison!"

On the main seat of the Tang Family, Tang Qingshan was stern without being angry, his tone icy.

But the Family Head's eyes were always fixed on the young man's right arm before him.

The young man's right arm was noticeably much larger than his left, a disproportionate sight that looked somewhat horrifying.

The young man named Tang Liu stood tall and proud, not fearing Tang Qingshan's authority in the slightest, and sneered:

"Haha! If you want to condemn someone, you can always find an excuse!"

"My father saved more than ten Tang Family clansmen a few days ago, and even lost his life for it!"

"Today, you want my life too?"

Tang Qingshan's eyes narrowed slightly, and he said coldly:

"Men! Throw him into the death prison!"

Immediately, two Tang Family clansmen stepped forward to escort Tang Liu away.

One of them said with a fake smile:

"Liuzi, you secretly learned the Family's secret technique; everyone knows about this. Not being executed on the spot is already an extraordinary act of grace."

The other person had a playful expression, "Hehe, didn't your father leave you a trident when he died? I heard it's a Spirit Rank weapon? Since you're going to the death prison anyway, why not give it to me from now on?"

Tang Liu merely gave them a cold glance and said nothing.

"Slap!"

A slap landed squarely on Tang Liu's face!

"What kind of look is that, you bastard? Do you believe I won't cripple you?"

"Hmm?" Tang Qingshan glanced at the two, who immediately wilted and quickly escorted Tang Liu away.

However, just as the two Tang Family clansmen were escorting Tang Liu past the Tang Family gate, Tang Liu suddenly erupted!

A three-pronged trident suddenly appeared in his hand!

He ruthlessly aimed at one person's neck and stabbed down!

"Pfft!"

Blood splattered instantly!

Then Tang Liu unhesitatingly crashed through the Tang Family gate.

"Bang!"

After crashing through the gate, Tang Liu rolled a few times on the ground, then quickly got up and ran rapidly towards outside the city.

Not long after, an angry roar came from within the Tang Manor:

"The rebellious son of the Tang Family, Tang Liu, harmed a fellow clansman, and from today onwards, he is removed from the Tang Family name!"

"Any Tang Family Disciple may kill him!"

The next moment, more than ten Cultivators rushed out of the Tang Family gate, frantically chasing Tang Liu.

At this time, Tang Liu had already rushed out of Biluo Town!

Going back a few days, Tang Liu and his father led Tang Family Disciples out to sea to hunt Spirit Beasts for the Tang Family, but they unexpectedly encountered a Spirit Spring Realm sea beast!

Tang Liu's father, as the leader, resolutely protected the Tang Family Disciples behind him, directly burning his essence blood to fight the Spirit Spring Realm sea beast to a mutual destruction with his Meridian Opening Sixth Realm!

Only when everyone brought Tang Liu's father and the Spirit Spring Realm sea beast's corpse back to the Tang Family did they realize that the Spirit Spring Realm sea beast had already been severely wounded!

A Spirit Rank weapon was stuck inside its body, which was precisely the trident in Tang Liu's hand!

Logically speaking, Tang Liu's father had risked his life to protect the Tang Family Disciples, and for the Tang Family, he had gained the corpse of a Spirit Spring Realm sea beast plus a Spirit Rank weapon. His contributions should have been enough to ensure Tang Liu's carefree life in the Tang Family from then on.

However, Tang Qingshan used the excuse of Tang Liu secretly learning the Tang Family's secret technique to throw him into the death prison!

But Tang Liu had accidentally overheard the real reason, which was his right arm!

...Meanwhile, Chu Xian and Big Yellow traveled through various towns and villages along the East Sea Coast.

Chu Xian's steps were a bit erratic, and he was panting heavily from exhaustion:

"Big Yellow, how many villages have we visited?"

Big Yellow barked once: "Woof!"

Then he scratched the ground again with his paw.

"What? It's already the eleventh? We haven't even recruited a single person?"

At first, Chu Xian had wanted to recruit some talented young geniuses, but after being beaten down by society, Chu Xian now had only one requirement for recruiting Disciples:

As long as they're human!

There was no other reason; no one had ever heard of the Lingxiao Sect, and they didn't know where this small, obscure Sect came from.

Furthermore, the East Sea Coast frequently experienced sea beast attacks, and those sea beasts specifically hunted Cultivators!

Although ordinary people suffered more casualties during beast tides, they were mostly trampled to death, and as long as they hid in underground tunnels, there generally wouldn't be any major problems.

And the many small Families along the East Sea Coast, for the survival of their Families, all had their own unique ways of preserving their lives.

In contrast, those Sects, especially some newly established small Sects, mostly became prey for sea beasts.

In people's eyes, the Lingxiao Sect belonged to the category of small Sects.

Therefore, it was not at all surprising that Chu Xian hadn't recruited a single person.

As one person and one dog walked along the road, a voice suddenly sounded in Chu Xian's ear:

"Reporting, Sect Master, a young girl has arrived outside the Sect, claiming she wishes to join the Sect. May I ask if the Sect Master permits it?"

The owner of the voice was none other than Red Feather.

Chu Xian was overjoyed to hear that someone wanted to join the Sect!

"Good, good, good! This Sect Master permits it! She will directly become a Direct Disciple!"

"Give her all the best resources in the entire Sect!"

Chu Xian was truly too happy!

He was almost as exhausted as Big Yellow, and hadn't recruited anyone, yet now someone had actually come to join the Sect on their own!

According to Red Feather, it was even a Female Disciple!

Happiness came too suddenly!

Seeing the sun about to set, Chu Xian jumped onto Big Yellow's back and waved his hand:

"Big Yellow! Back to the Sect to see the Disciple!"

Under the temptation of a few pieces of dog food, Big Yellow was incredibly excited!

"Woof woof!"

One person and one dog transformed into a wisp of smoke and rushed towards the Lingxiao Sect.



6: Half-Emperor Lin Xuan ascends to the Heavenly Palace!

Before the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate.

A young woman in green looked up at the three characters "Lingxiao Sect," pondering to herself.

"I never thought that I, a Half-Emperor, would have a day of Rebirth."

"Heh heh! You dog man andcheap woman, you two must live well! Wait until I personally grind your bones to dust!"

"The Spiritual Qi here is quite rich. If I seclude myself here for twenty years to Cultivate, I will be invincible upon emerging!"

Lin Xuan, a Half-Emperor from another continent, was betrayed by her best friend and Dao Companion, but unexpectedly underwent Rebirth on the Xuanxiao Continent.

As Lin Xuan was deep in thought, Red Feather, clad in red, appeared at the Mountain Gate.

"Come in."

Red Feather's words gave Lin Xuan an incredibly peaceful feeling, making her instinctively want to get close to him.

Lin Xuan was secretly startled:

"Hmm? This person's temperament is no less than my previous life's; he is definitely not a Mortal!"

"I never expected such a person to exist in this remote place!"

Lin Xuan appeared respectful:

"Yes."

The moment Lin Xuan's body crossed the Mountain Gate!

Buzz!

Lin Xuan felt an overwhelming surge of vast Spiritual Qi rush towards her face!

In an instant, Lin Xuan felt a multitude of natural laws appear before her eyes!

There were even a few sporadic, hard-to-capture streaks of light shimmering around her body!

Lin Xuan was certain that if she had Cultivated here in her previous life, she would have long since attained the Dao and become an Emperor!

"This place... could it be the legendary Immortal Realm?"

This former Half-Emperor was utterly stunned on the spot!

Lin Xuan: Is this right?

Is this still a low-level continent?

Is this a remote area near the East Sea?

Even the Holy Land she created in her previous life was merely average compared to the Cultivation environment before her, wasn't it?

Red Feather's crimson lips parted slightly: "Don't just stand there, follow this Elder."

"The Sect Master has decreed that you are specially permitted to be a Personal Disciple. All resources of the Sect are available for your use."

Lin Xuan: Hmm!

Hmm?

Reaching the Peak upon entry?

Directly becoming the Sect Master's Direct Disciple!

Cultivation resources available for free use!

Is this the cheat-like life after Rebirth?

If I had known it would be like this, why didn't this Emperor undergo Rebirth sooner?

Lin Xuan followed Red Feather deeper into the Lingxiao Sect. As they went further, this former Half-Emperor finally thoroughly understood just how outrageous the Sect she was now in truly was!

Saint-Grade fruit trees and Saint-Grade medicine could be described as ubiquitous!

What was even more outrageous!

Tiny runes were engraved on every Sect building, and these numerous runes formed one killing array after another!

Lin Xuan was secretly terrified:

With such a scale of killing arrays, even with her Half-Emperor Cultivation in her previous life, she probably wouldn't last a single stick of incense before perishing!

And this wasn't even the most terrifying aspect of this Sect!

The most terrifying thing was that, apart from the Elder before her, she only sensed the aura of one other Cultivator!

A Sect with such profound heritage must have countless powerful experts within!

But why did she only sense one Meridian Connecting Realm Cultivator?

That must mean the others had already reached a Realm she couldn't perceive!

For example, this woman before her, in her estimation, was at least a Great Saint!

Although Lin Xuan was currently only at Qi Condensation second level, with her experience and knowledge as a Half-Emperor in her previous life, Cultivators below the Saint-Grade, unless they deliberately concealed themselves, could still be sensed by Lin Xuan.

"Elder, Disciple has a question I'd like to ask."

Huh? Such an awesome Sect only has four people? And that includes me, who just joined?

What about all the experts I just imagined?

Strange! Strange!

Yu Jian simultaneously broke through three minor Realms and one major Realm!

His current Cultivation had already reached Spiritual Spring first level!

7: I've never seen such a casual sect before!

Chu Xian rode Big Yellow, galloping all the way towards Lingxiao Sect, when suddenly the System's notification sound rang in his ears.

What Chu Xian didn't know was that four of these ten lottery chances were provided by Lin Xuan, who had just joined the Sect!

Lin Xuan, who had just joined the Sect, broke through from Qi Condensation second level to Qi Condensation sixth level within a few hours in the Lingxiao Sect!

“Ten-draw in progress!”

“This ten-draw guarantees an Emperor-Grade reward!”

When Chu Xian got closer, he saw a young man covered in blood, like a blood gourd!

A layer of blood-mud covered his slightly tender face, and his chest was extensively caved in!

“Hiss!”

“Save him!”

As soon as the Medicinal Pill was consumed, the young man's wounds began to heal rapidly at a visible speed!

In just a few breaths, all the young man's wounds had recovered!

“Not bad! The System's stuff is really good!”

Seeing no sign of the young man waking up, Chu Xian threw him onto Big Yellow's back and continued towards the Lingxiao Sect.

Finally, late at night, Chu Xian and Big Yellow returned to the Lingxiao Sect.

After settling the young man, Chu Xian finally had time to call his two Personal Disciples over.

Yu Jian was the first to speak: “Disciple greets Revered Master!”

Lin Xuan, on the other hand, was staring intently at Chu Xian.

She was observing Chu Xian, and Chu Xian was also observing her.

Lin Xuan's eyebrows were like spring mountains, her eyes held autumn water, and a hint of sternness shone in her gaze.

Her dark hair was simply tied up, with a few strands falling by her ears.

Her green robes were well-tailored, the skirt swaying gently, moving like willows in spring.

Although it was late at night, the entire Main Peak was not dim at all.

The light emitted by various crystals shone on Lin Xuan, making the girl's temperament appear even more noble.

But in Lin Xuan's eyes, Chu Xian was only one word.

Handsome!

Other than being handsome, he was useless!

Yu Jian quietly elbowed Lin Xuan, squeezing out a voice through clenched teeth:

Lin Xuan finally reacted, quickly bowing and saying:

“Disciple Lin Xuan greets Revered Master!”

Chu Xian nodded in satisfaction, “Hmm, not bad. From now on, you two must help each other. If you have any questions about Cultivation, feel free to ask me.”

Lin Xuan secretly pouted: I'm a Half-Emperor, do I still need to ask you?

Revered Master, you should go somewhere cool and stay there!

“Oh, right, since everyone is here, let me introduce someone to you.”

As soon as his voice fell, a person instantly appeared beside Chu Xian!

This scene immediately made Lin Xuan's pupils contract!

Spatial Law!

Could this person also be in the Great Sage Realm?

Little did she know, this person, like Red Feather, was also a genuine Emperor Realm expert!

In Lin Xuan's consciousness, the Great Emperor Realm was the highest Realm she could imagine, and also her highest pursuit in both her past and present lives.

So she would never associate Red Feather and the person in front of her with the words Great Emperor.

“This is someone I met while out today. He wants to find a meal at my Lingxiao Sect, so from now on, he will be the Second Elder of my Lingxiao Sect.”

“Elder Up and Down, say hello to everyone.”

The middle-aged man beside Chu Xian had a constant smile on his face. Hearing Chu Xian's words, he smiled and said:

“Hello everyone, my name is Up and Down. Thanks to the Sect Master's appreciation, if anyone needs anything in the future, just ask.”

After the introductions, Chu Xian took out two sets of Sect robes and two Direct Disciples tokens from the System space, handed them to Yu Jian and the other, and sent them back.

Then he glanced at the unconscious young man again and fell into a deep sleep.

Chu Xian was currently just a Mortal. Staying up half the night made him feel exhausted.

The next morning.

Chu Xian, who was sound asleep, was awakened by a shout!

“Hey! You stop right there! Your arm is swollen like a pig's trotters, why are you running?!”

At this moment, Yu Jian, holding a wooden basin, was frantically chasing someone on the Main Peak.

Ahead of Yu Jian, the young man who had been unconscious yesterday was vigorously running, swinging his right arm, which was as thick as his thigh!

Lin Xuan was also called out by Yu Jian's shouting. She glared at Yu Jian in annoyance, then kicked the stone stool in front of her towards the young man.

“Whoosh!”

“Thud!”

The stone stool, making a whooshing sound, directly hit the young man's face!

The young man's attention was entirely on Yu Jian behind him. He didn't notice the stone stool flying towards him and was directly hit, leaving him dizzy and disoriented!

“Didn't that solve it? So much trouble!”

Lin Xuan mumbled, then turned and went back to her room to continue Cultivating.

No one noticed that Lin Xuan's Cultivation had already reached the Peak of Qi Condensation!

From Lin Xuan's entry into the Sect until now, in just one day and one night, this Rebirth Half-Emperor had directly broken through from Qi Condensation second level to the Peak of Qi Condensation!

Her Cultivation speed could only be described as terrifying!

Chu Xian, with panda eyes, came closer, “What's all this noise about?”

Yu Jian helped the young man up and replied:

“Revered Master, I saw his arm was swollen like this and wanted to wipe it with medicinal liquid, but this kid slapped me and ran off!”

“Look at my face, the handprint is still there!”

Sure enough, Yu Jian's already not-so-handsome face now had a large, red handprint!

“Uh... this kid hits hard!”

Chu Xian grimaced!

After ordering Yu Jian to seal the young man's Cultivation, Chu Xian and the other two went to the Main Hall.

Chu Xian sat in the high seat and asked the young man:

“Boy, you fell from the cliff, and I saved your life. Are you willing to join my Lingxiao Sect?”

In Chu Xian's heart, nothing was more important than taking in Disciples!

As for the young man's background?

It didn't matter at all!

Even if you have some schemes, my Lingxiao Sect has two Great Emperors guarding it. Who can steal anything?

Chu Xian's question completely stunned Tang Liu, who had run away from home!

Lingxiao Sect?

He had never heard of it!

Besides, this Sect takes Disciples without even asking their name, checking their talent, or caring about their Heart-Nature, just accepting them directly?

He had never seen such a casual Sect!



8: Tang Liu entered the sect! Marine life!

This young man was none other than Tang Liu, who was being hunted by the Tang Family!

When Tang Liu woke up, he saw Yu Jian fumbling on his right arm and, thinking it was a Tang Family clansman, caused a misunderstanding.

Now that he knew the person in front of him had no ill intent, he lowered his guard.

Hearing that Lingxiao Sect intended to take him in, Tang Liu's heart was slightly touched.

Tang Liu's current situation left him with no choice but to join Lingxiao Sect.

All Tang Family clansman had been dispatched to hunt him down; yesterday, he was forced to jump off a cliff!

Fortunately, he wasn't fated to die and met Chu Xian!

"Kid, speak up! Do you want to join my Lingxiao Sect and become my Personal Disciple?"

Yu Jian grew impatient waiting on the side. He had already been slapped by Tang Liu for no reason, and seeing the kid hesitate to answer his Revered Master's question, he directly slapped Tang Liu on the back!

Tang Liu winced in pain!

He glared at Yu Jian in annoyance, "Disciple Tang Liu greets Revered Master!"
