"After you return, you must hang Tang Qingshan's head on the city wall of Biluo City. I want him to never enter Reincarnation!"

Tang Liu had once heard his father say that humans have three gates, namely Body, Will, and Soul.

After a person dies, the three gates open simultaneously: the Body enters the earth, the Will enters the void, and the Soul enters the heavens, only then can they enter Reincarnation.

Body represents the Physical Body. The head is the controller of all bodily actions. As long as the head is not buried in the earth, this person will not enter Reincarnation.

The Physical Body will merge with the earth, and the Divine Soul will pass through the Soul Gate into the spatial rift, forever enduring the suffering of being trampled by all living beings.

Will represents the Will. If a person has an incredibly strong Will at the time of death, this Will will remain in the world, and the Divine Soul will pass through the Soul Gate into the spatial rift.

Soul represents the soul. Great powers who Cultivate to a certain Realm have immensely powerful Divine Souls and can close the Soul Gate themselves, allowing their Divine Souls to remain in the world.



12: Somewhat famous, he's got the makings of a movie star!

The Tang Family's matter could be considered concluded. The Tang Family clansmen carried Tang Qingshan's corpse back to the Tang Family and hung his head on Biluo City's wall.

Chu Xian used the lottery chances he had accumulated over the past two days. Seeing that there was nothing good, he led Big Yellow out of the Sect again.

This time, Chu Xian's goal was clear: to go to Biluo City to recruit disciples!

The Tang Family was the ruler of Biluo City, and a well-known power along the East Sea Coast.

However, the Tang Family Patriarch died at the entrance of the newly established Lingxiao Sect, which was simply a Divine-Grade advertisement for the Lingxiao Sect!

When Chu Xian arrived at the gate of Biluo City, Tang Qingshan's head was already hanging high.

"Liu'er's heart is still too soft!"

"The Tang Family clansmen initially wanted to kill you, but you let them all go..."

"Let your master clean up your mess."

Chu Xian stroked Big Yellow's head and ordered, "Big Yellow, I'll leave this to you!"

Big Yellow: "Woof!"

Big Yellow had been soaking in the bloodline immortal pool and devouring the dog food obtained from the System's lottery for two days!

Now, his Realm had broken through three more levels, reaching the eighth stage of Meridional Communication!

In Biluo City, Big Yellow was more than capable of walking around as he pleased!

After glancing at the rapidly departing Big Yellow, Chu Xian walked through the streets of Biluo City and couldn't help but sigh with emotion:

"The Tang Family's governance of the city is actually quite good."

Arriving at the market street, Chu Xian finally heard the news he wanted to hear.

A group of people were discussing.

"The Tang Family has ruled Biluo City for so many years, and it's been pretty good. I never thought Patriarch Qingshan would die."

"You don't know, do you? Tang Qingshan deserved his death!"

"Oh? You know? Tell us quickly!"

"Haha! Good! Then I'll tell everyone!"

"My third uncle's cousin's thirteenth aunt and Tang Qingshan of the Tang Family are sworn brothers! This news is absolutely true!"

"So, Tang Qingshan..."

This person was surrounded by a group of people, telling the story so vividly, as if he had witnessed it himself.

"Speaking of Tang Qingshan, he was truly not a good person. He even dared to eat people! What's more, he even ate his own Family clansmen!"

"That's right! Fortunately, someone from the Lingxiao Sect took action, otherwise, if Tang Qingshan had eaten all the Tang Family members, what would have happened?"

"Lingxiao Sect? I've never heard of this Sect before!"

"It might be a new Sect, but from the looks of it, this Sect should be very powerful!"

"Alas, no matter how powerful, they still can't avoid being eaten by sea beasts in the end!"

"That's right! There used to be powerful Sects, but the ultimate result was that they all died out."

Chu Xian listened intently from the side, but found that the topic was getting increasingly off-kilter.

These guys still had no intention of joining the Lingxiao Sect?

Chu Xian quickly pretended to be an ordinary person and joined the discussion circle.

"In my opinion! This Lingxiao Sect can completely solve the sea beast riot!"

"A few days ago, I went hunting in the mountains and unknowingly arrived at the entrance of that Lingxiao Sect."

"Guess what?"

Everyone immediately became curious!

They were all Mortals, full of endless curiosity about Cultivation, and as soon as they heard someone had personally seen a so-called Sect, their eyes immediately filled with fervor!

Everyone had fantasized about one day being able to fly into the sky and burrow into the earth, being omnipotent, but due to sea beasts specifically preying on Cultivators, they dared not embark on the path of Cultivation.

The person who had been telling the story earlier also became interested and said excitedly:

"Brother! What's the Lingxiao Sect like? Tell us!"

To integrate into the group, Chu Xian immediately became an actor:

"Hehe, I'm just a rough person, but I just think this Sect is too awesome!"

"The Sect's gate was wide open, with clouds and mist swirling around. The peaks inside soared into the clouds, and spiritual light shot to the sky! I even personally saw someone flying in the sky!"

The people around them all pricked up their ears!

"What? Can fly? Holy crap!"

"Awesome! That's not a person anymore, definitely an Immortal!"

"Right, right, right, I heard from my third uncle's cousin's thirteenth aunt that Cultivators can fly when they reach a certain Realm! Definitely an Immortal without a doubt!"

Everyone had seen Cultivators, but they had never seen Cultivators who could fly. As soon as they heard that someone in the Lingxiao Sect could fly, they immediately identified them as Immortals.

"So, this Sect is really powerful?"

Chu Xian continued to boast:

"Isn't it? I was watching with great interest then, and I guess I was discovered by the Immortals inside. Mist immediately rose around me! After the mist cleared, guess what happened?"

"Were you taken into the Sect?"

"What's it like inside the Lingxiao Sect?"

Chu Xian smiled bitterly and shook his head, continuing:

"How could an Immortal let me in? After the mist cleared, I found myself directly back in our Biluo City!"

"I had walked for two days to reach the entrance of that Lingxiao Sect, but I didn't expect to be sent back in less than a quarter of an hour!"

The surrounding crowd gasped in unison!

"Ah? A two-day journey, and you were sent back in less than a quarter of an hour? Is that an Immortal's Spell?"

"That's too amazing, isn't it? If I learned Spells, I could go to the county city to sell goods, and a round trip that usually takes a month would take less than half a day!"

"Big brother, you're learning Spells to sell goods? That's such a woman's view!"

"If I learned Spells, I would definitely visit all the brothels in this world!"

"Bah! You lecher! If I could use Spells..."

Everyone chattered about what they would do after learning an Immortal's Spell, while Chu Xian just listened with a chuckle.

Just then, a shocking shout instantly echoed throughout Biluo City!

"Ah! Something terrible has happened! The Tang Family was actually wiped out by a dog!"

As soon as these words came out, they instantly ignited the entire Biluo City!

"What? A single dog wiped out the Tang Family?"

"Go quickly and see! It might be the Lingxiao Sect's Immortal Dog!"

People rushed towards the Tang Family, Chu Xian smiled slightly and followed the crowd towards the Tang Family.

Arriving at the Tang Family's entrance, they saw Big Yellow sitting majestically on the Tang Family's gate, with an extraordinary Aura!

Chu Xian seized the opportunity to exclaim:

"Damn! It really is the Lingxiao Sect's Immortal Dog! I saw a yellow dog in the Lingxiao Sect that day!"

Subsequently, the residents of Biluo City became even more frantic!

"A single dog wiped out the entire Tang Family? That's too fierce, isn't it?"

"If this is true, I'm willing to be a dog in the Lingxiao Sect!"

"..."

As the residents exclaimed, Big Yellow leaped directly from the gate and quickly headed out of the city.

Seeing this, Chu Xian quietly withdrew from the crowd.

Arriving at the city gate, Big Yellow emerged from a corner and came to Chu Xian with a triumphant look.

Chu Xian smiled and took out two dog treats, throwing them to Big Yellow.

Just as the man and dog were about to leave the city!

The entire Biluo City suddenly began to shake violently!

What's going on?

An earthquake?

Can the Xuanxiao Continent even have earthquakes?

Chu Xian was a little confused. He had Transmigrated for more than twenty years and had never encountered an earthquake.

Big Yellow bared his teeth and barked wildly, his gaze fixed on the area outside the city!

"Woof woof woof woof!"

The residents at the city gate immediately sprang into action upon seeing this situation!

"Sea beasts are attacking the city! Retreat quickly!"



13: Sea monster crisis!

Chu Xian didn't know what had happened, but the residents of Biluo City did.

The moment the ground trembled, the people within Biluo City sprang into action!

Dozens of them ran, beating gongs with a 'clang, clang, clang,' shouting as they went.

"Sea beasts attacking the city! Evacuate quickly!"

"Sea beasts attacking the city! Everyone, evacuate quickly!"

The people within Biluo City appeared incredibly chaotic, but upon closer inspection, it wasn't hard to see that everyone was moving rapidly according to some order!

In just the time it takes to steep a cup of tea, Biluo City seemed to transform into an empty city, its once bustling streets deserted!

Chu Xian raised an eyebrow slightly and murmured:

"To achieve such an effect, Biluo City must have experienced countless such situations before!"

"Sea beasts attacking the city!"

This couldn't help but remind Chu Xian of the incident he experienced three years ago.

"Three years ago, I lost my cultivation and my parents in a beast tide."

"Now, let these beasts be the stepping stone for Lingxiao Sect to gain fame!"

Chu Xian took out an exquisite jade stone from his embrace and held it in his hand, his gaze directed outside Biluo City.

At the edge of his vision, towering waves, thousands of feet high, carried dense formations of scaled beasts rapidly towards Biluo City!

At the very front, a gigantic crab charged swiftly, followed closely by a dense tide of beasts!

The ground rumbled under the trampling of the beast tide, and coupled with the crashing of the waves, Biluo City seemed to have arrived at the end of the world!

Da Huang had lost his earlier arrogance and was now hiding behind Chu Xian with his tail between his legs.

Chu Xian stroked Da Huang's head with his left hand, and with a forceful squeeze of his right palm, the jade stone in his hand instantly shattered!

"Sect Master, what are your orders?"

The voices of Red Feather and Up and Down appeared simultaneously in Chu Xian's mind!

"Bad luck, I encountered a sea beast attack on Biluo City. You two handle it, and send Yu Jian over as well."

"As you command!"

"Yes!"

The next moment!

A colossal hand slowly emerged in the sky outside the city!

The firmament suddenly darkened, and the azure-golden giant hand tore through the Void Realm, with remnants of the Star River coiling around its knuckles!

The giant hand gently caressed, and a fierce wind suddenly rose!

The towering waves, thousands of feet high, were actually blown into a gap by the fierce wind!

The beast tide was even blown into large empty spaces by the fierce wind!

Immediately after, the giant hand suddenly clenched, and the gigantic crab leading the charge was lifted into the air by an invisible force!

"Bang!"

The crab was instantly crushed!

Blood mixed with seawater cascaded down with a 'gurgle,' momentarily causing the beast tide to halt its advance!

However, a violent spiritual power shot out from the ocean, instantly merging into the towering waves where the sea beast tide was located.

The beast tide advanced again!

"Rumble!"

The giant hand in the sky seemed to be enraged!

Its palm instantly pressed down!

The waves where the beast tide was located immediately caved in, forming a gigantic palm print!

And the hundred thousand sea beasts within those waves were all crushed into bloody mist under this colossal palm!

The towering waves, thousands of feet high, turned blood-red in the blink of an eye!

The giant palm in the air moved again!

With a swing, the blood-colored waves surged back into the ocean, and the previously submerged land didn't even retain a single drop of seawater!

Then, the giant palm appeared above the great sea, transforming into a golden light that plunged into the ocean!

"Plop!"

Stirring up endless splashes!

Not long after, the golden light enveloped a thousand-zhang-long Dragon and leaped out of the sea!

The Dragon spoke human words, struggling and shouting:

"Who is it? How dare you kill my subjects and ruin my Dao of becoming a Dragon?"

However, no one responded, and the Dragon was carried away by the golden giant hand, like a chick.

In Biluo City, Yu Jian had already appeared at the city gate.

"Revered Master, you called me."

Chu Xian nodded, explained the general situation to Yu Jian, and then instructed:

"Go tell the people of Biluo City that my Lingxiao Sect has resolved the sea beast problem, and there will be no more sea beast attacks in the future. Also, tell them the location of my Lingxiao Sect."

Immediately, Chu Xian and Da Huang headed towards Lingxiao Sect.

Yu Jian arrived at the center of Biluo City. Now in the Spirit Spring Realm, he circulated his spiritual power to amplify his voice and shouted loudly:

"Hey, hey, hey! The sea beasts have been dealt with by my Lingxiao Sect! Everyone can come out now!"

"From now on, there will be no more sea beast attacks!"

"Everyone no longer needs to worry!"

"If anyone wishes to join Lingxiao Sect, they can travel two hundred li to the northwest to reach it!"

Yu Jian's voice, amplified by spiritual power, spread rapidly throughout Biluo City.

The residents of Biluo City, deep underground, heard it clearly.

"The sea beasts are dealt with?"

"Huh? It seems to be true! The earthquake has stopped!"

"You all stay hidden, I'll go out and see!"

The underground was brightly lit at this time, and although there were many people, it didn't feel crowded at all.

It was evident that the residents of Biluo City had meticulously crafted these underground passages.

Everyone looked solemn and admonished, "Be careful, Ergou!"

Not long after, Ergou ran back, panting.

"The sea beasts... are gone!"

"Everyone... everyone can go out."

After receiving confirmation, the residents of Biluo City began to emerge from the underground passages in an orderly fashion.

When people saw that the entire city was intact, they couldn't help but show excited expressions!

"It seems the sea beasts really were driven away! Every time they used to destroy half the houses, but this time even the city walls aren't damaged!" one person exclaimed, pointing at the city walls.

What they called city walls were actually just small earthen walls about two people high.

Each time the sea beasts attacked, they would cause extensive damage to the city walls, so some towns along the East Sea Coast simply built their city walls out of earth.

"Who was the person shouting just now? They said Lingxiao Sect solved the sea beasts?"

"We must find them quickly; we need to thank them properly!"

The residents of Biluo City wanted to find Yu Jian, but they didn't know that Yu Jian had already left Biluo City and was currently traveling with Chu Xian and Da Huang.

The residents of Biluo City searched for a long time but didn't even see a single person.

People couldn't help but start making all sorts of guesses about Lingxiao Sect.

At this moment, the few people who had conversed with Chu Xian earlier began to play their part!

"Lingxiao Sect! Oh, I know all about it!"

"It's a new Sect that just established here!"

Thus, the name of Lingxiao Sect, with the help of these people, quickly spread throughout Biluo City!

"Alright! I need to go home and take my son to Lingxiao Sect to become a disciple! Everyone, please help yourselves!"

"Hey, hey, hey! Brother, how about we go together? I'm also taking my kid to see!"

"That's great! We'll set off after lunch!"

"Count me in! Count me in!"

"I'm going too!"

"..."

Just after noon, a large crowd had gathered outside Biluo City, at least ten thousand people!

Most of them were teenagers and middle-aged people, mostly parents leading their children, with only a few elderly individuals.

"Hey? Who was that hunter from this morning? He's been to Lingxiao Sect, he should lead the way!"

"Haven't seen that guy at all, could he have gone ahead already?"

"In that case, let's set off! Everyone, make sure to keep up and don't fall behind!"

Just as the people were excitedly about to depart, a green beam of light was seen flying rapidly from afar!

Before long, the green light landed in front of the crowd!

The newcomer stood on a giant leaf, his expression stern, and he questioned sharply:

"Bold commoners! Who allowed so many of you to leave the town at once?"

"Return to the city immediately!"

"Otherwise! Kill without mercy!"



14: Dragon Pride? Mount!

The person came with a long sword on his back, stepping on a huge green leaf, his eyes revealing a cold light.

He was wearing a black robe with a large 'Xia' character embroidered on his chest.

The residents of Biluo City recognized the newcomer at a glance.

It was an Imperial Guard from the Great Xia Empire's East Sea Coast region.

Because the East Sea Coast was constantly attacked by sea beasts, the Great Xia Imperial Family, in order to prevent residents of the East Sea Coast from migrating to other areas and causing the sea beasts to advance inland, specially dispatched Imperial Guards to supervise this region.

An old man quickly stepped forward to explain:

"Imperial Guard Senior, we are not trying to immigrate, but rather plan to take our children to Lingxiao Sect to seek an Opportunity."

The Imperial Guard frowned slightly, "Lingxiao Sect?"

"Yes, Lingxiao Sect is a new School that repelled the beast tide for our Biluo City, which is why we want our children to try their luck at Lingxiao Sect, to find an Opportunity, not to immigrate."

The Imperial Guard's brows immediately twisted into a knot!

A new School!

It meant that the number of people in the School would not be too many.

But it was able to repel a beast tide!

Then how powerful must this new School be?

Most importantly, he was in charge of this area, when did such a powerful School appear?

He hadn't heard a single whisper!

If his superior found out about this, it would be a matter of losing his head!

"Are you lying to me?"

"To repel a beast tide, that is not something a small, new School can accomplish!"

The old man quickly knelt, "Imperial Guard Senior, how could this old man lie to you? If you don't believe me, come with us and you'll see!"

The Imperial Guard was skeptical but dared not be careless.

If this new School, Lingxiao Sect, really had the power to repel the beast tide, he must report it to his superior immediately!

"Lead the way!"

The Imperial Guard jumped off the large green leaf, and with a gentle swipe of his palm, he put the large green leaf into his nascent void ring.

Many residents of Biluo City breathed a sigh of relief when they saw that the Imperial Guard had no intention of blaming them for now.

The few people who had initially talked with Chu Xian quickly stepped forward to lead the way, and the surging crowd formed a long Dragon, slowly moving towards Lingxiao Sect...

Above Lingxiao Sect's Main Peak, Chu Xian and Yu Jian had already returned.

Red Feather was constantly swinging the Dragon from the sea in her hand; its original thousand-zhang-long body was now only about half a meter long.

"Ah ya ya! Granny, spare my life~"

The Dragon spoke human words, already dizzy from Red Feather's swinging, and could only beg bitterly.

Seeing Chu Xian return, Red Feather, clutching the Dragon, gracefully walked over.

"Sect Master, you're back? How was the harvest?"

The Dragon's pupils contracted!

Sect Master?

This red-clothed woman was already an Emperor Realm, and she still had a Sect Master above her?

Then what kind of strength must this Sect Master possess?

Chu Xian came and sat down in his chair, feeling exhausted.

"This time, it should attract many people to join my Lingxiao Sect."

"Red Feather, you and Up and Down discuss and set up an entrance trial for the Sect; you can do it this way..."

Chu Xian meticulously explained his ideas to Red Feather; as for the specific plan, it would be jointly discussed by the two of them.

After giving his instructions, Chu Xian finally turned his gaze to the small Dragon.

At this moment, the Dragon was completely terrified!

That red-clothed woman was a real Emperor!

Abusing him, a Great Sage Realm, was simply a flick of her hand!

But!

That woman had adopted the lowest posture towards this Sect Master!

What did this mean?

Had this man in front of him already surpassed the Emperor Realm?

Oh my heavens!

"Senior! I know my sins are profound! Please, Senior, give me a chance to mend my ways!"

"I am willing to stay in Lingxiao Sect and work like an ox or a horse! I only ask, Senior, to spare my life!"

Chu Xian closed his eyes, not even looking at the Dragon; this trip to Biluo City was truly too tiring, and Chu Xian's Mortal body was truly unable to bear it.

But in the Dragon's eyes, the nature of this was completely different!

Look!

Is this the demeanor of a powerful being?

He doesn't even need to use his eyes to look at people!

"You still know your sins are profound? Give me a reason not to kill you."

The Dragon immediately wanted to cry without tears!

"Senior! I've been wronged!"

"Don't think that every time I control sea beasts to attack cities, I never indiscriminately kill innocents!"

"These nearby towns, I specifically pick Cultivators to eat! Ordinary people, unless their luck is too bad, will not suffer any casualties."

"And those Cultivators are all cunning and treacherous people!"

"For example, the Tang Family of Biluo City! That old fellow Tang Qingshan has secretly used my subordinates to harm people many times!"

"So I started targeting the Tang Family. A few days ago, I went to the Tang Family to ask for someone, and he promised well on the surface, but who knew he would stand me up!"

"In a fit of rage, I then had the sea beasts attack the city to demand an explanation from the Tang Family!"

"Tang Qingshan! Hmph! If I get the chance, I will definitely eat him!"

Chu Xian did not open his eyes, and said calmly: "Tang Qingshan is dead."

The Dragon's small eyes spun around, and it asked softly: "You killed him?"

"Not me, it was my Disciple, Tang Liu."

The Dragon was instantly overjoyed!

"Wahahaha! Well killed, wonderfully killed! Even if your Disciple didn't kill him, I would have!"

After Chu Xian recovered some of his strength, he finally opened his eyes, "I can spare your life."

"I don't care if you used to eat good people or bad people, nor do I care what your purpose was in eating Cultivators. I will temporarily believe what you said."

"But if you let me know you have any ill intentions, you know the consequences."

"From now on, you will stay in Lingxiao Sect and be my mount."

The Dragon was listening intently, nodding continuously:

"Mm! Mm mm! Mm mm mm!"

But when it heard Chu Xian say he wanted it to be his mount, its mind suddenly went blank!

Make me a mount?

Did I hear that right?

Who is this person in front of me?

That is an existence that even an Emperor must obey!

I'm going to be his mount!

Did happiness come so suddenly?

"Senior..."

Chu Xian said: "What? Unwilling?"

"No, no! A thousand times willing!"

"I just want to ask... Am I worthy?"

Chu Xian couldn't help but feel a little amused!

"Stop talking nonsense! Establish the contract!"

The Dragon dared not say anything more, and joyfully constructed a master-servant contract with its Divine Soul, sending it before Chu Xian.

Hey! To be a mount for a powerful being above an Emperor!

It's simply the good fortune I've cultivated for eight lifetimes!

In the mount world from now on, I'll be the most awesome existence!

Chu Xian placed his palm on the phantom in front of him, and the phantom instantly merged into Chu Xian's body.

Immediately, a System pop-up appeared before Chu Xian's eyes:

【Congratulations to Host for subduing a mount!】

【Reward: Mount Feed × 100】

【Obtained title: Dragon Rider!】

【Mount Information: Dragon (Dragon Transformation Progress 39.9%)】

【Name: Long Aotian】

【Cultivation: Great Sage Fifth Level】

Chu Xian's heart was slightly pleased: "Great Sage Fifth Level! Good, good! Finally, I won't have to walk out to recruit Disciples myself anymore!"

On the other hand, Long Aotian.

The sky fell!

Only arrogance remained!

"What the hell! Not even Qi Condensation Realm? I..."

"Pfft!!!"

A mouthful of Dragon blood instantly spewed from Long Aotian's mouth!



15: A lucky draw? This sect is getting more and more casual about accepting disciples!

Time flew by, and two days later.

Over ten thousand people had gathered in front of the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate.

The residents of Biluo City, after two days of travel, finally arrived at the Lingxiao Sect's entrance.

Up and Down immediately reported the situation outside the Sect to Chu Xian.

Chu Xian looked at the multitude of people outside the Sect, and his heart was inevitably stirred!

It's been three years!

Do you know what I've been through these past three years?

Now, I finally don't have to be a cripple anymore!

Chu Xian took a deep breath and slowly said:

"Red Feather, how are the Sect entrance trials I assigned coming along?"

"Reporting to Sect Master, everything has been completed as per your instructions!"

"Good!"

"Order Yu Jian, Lin Xuan, and Tang Liu to leave the Sect and preside over the Disciple recruitment trials. Long Aotian is responsible for maintaining order. If anyone dares to cause trouble, just chop him down!"

Before long, the three Direct Disciples walked out of their rooms.

Yu Jian was full of passion, as if he approached everything with vigor!

Lin Xuan, on the other hand, looked disdainful, muttering softly:

"Hmph! Constantly interrupting my Cultivation! Stinky Revered Master, the first thing I'll do when I recover my cultivation is tie you up!"

After the Tang Family incident, Tang Liu's personality clearly became introverted. He approached Chu Xian with an expressionless face.

After Chu Xian explained the Disciple recruitment trials to the three, the Lingxiao Sect finally welcomed its first official Disciple recruitment!

Outside the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate.

The people of Biluo City were anxiously waiting when suddenly three figures appeared at the Lingxiao Sect's entrance.

"Eh? Young Master Tang Liu? Why is he at the Lingxiao Sect?"

"Ah! I know! Have you all heard about Tang Qingshan being killed by the Lingxiao Sect?"

"So that's it! The Tang Family pursued Tang Liu, Tang Liu entered the Lingxiao Sect, and then Tang Qingshan was killed!"

"The Lingxiao Sect is indeed powerful. They killed a figure like Tang Qingshan just like that! We really came to the right place!"

As everyone was chattering, a powerful voice suddenly boomed!

"Silence!"

Immediately after, a handsome middle-aged man slowly walked out from the Lingxiao Sect!

The person was dressed in a golden robe, embroidered with a five-clawed Golden Dragon!

It was Long Aotian in his human form.

This attire, however, made the Imperial Guard from the Great Xia Empire in the crowd narrow his eyes!

"This Lingxiao Sect is incredibly bold! Someone dares to wear a Dragon robe!"

"Let me see what strength this Lingxiao Sect truly possesses!"

Long Aotian waved his hand, and dozens of circular plates flew out from his sleeve with a whoosh!

After the large circular plates landed, everyone could clearly see what was in front of them.

There was a pointer in the middle of the circular plate, and the plate had five colors, which were:

White for no qualification,

Green for Utility Disciple qualification,

Blue for Outer Sect Disciple qualification,

Purple for Inner Sect Disciple qualification,

Gold for Personal Disciple qualification.

Among them, "no qualification" accounted for the largest proportion, a full 90%!

And the color for Personal Disciple was only a tiny sliver, less than 1% of the total!

Just as everyone was looking at the large spinning wheel in front of them, confused, Yu Jian came to a spinning wheel and said loudly:

"Everyone, my Revered Master already knows your intentions, and has specially introduced the Lingxiao Sect's unique Disciple recruitment method!"

"That is, it only looks at luck, not talent!"

"Below, I will explain!"

"This is our Sect's Disciple recruitment method! The Lucky Wheel!"

"Those participating in the Disciple recruitment ceremony only need to step forward and spin the wheel. Wherever the pointer ultimately lands, that will be the result!"

As he spoke, Yu Jian suddenly spun the wheel!

"Buzz!"

Yu Jian used too much force, and the wheel began to spin rapidly. Due to the high speed, the wheel in everyone's eyes seemed to only show the most common white color!

People waited patiently for the wheel to stop, but their hearts were already impatient!

They had never heard of such a Disciple recruitment method before!

It actually only looked at luck, not talent!

This was too reckless!

The residents of Biluo City originally wanted their children to enter the Sect to Cultivate, but they never expected the Lingxiao Sect's Disciple recruitment method to be like this!

Doesn't that mean they could also try?

The Imperial Guard in the crowd, however, had his eyebrows twisted in anger!

What the hell!

What kind of bizarre Sect is this?

Their Disciple recruitment method is too trendy!

Are they implying they have too many resources?

If you have too many, can you give me some?

As everyone waited with bated breath, the spinning wheel in front of Yu Jian finally stopped!

Under everyone's eager gaze, the pointer landed perfectly on that tiny sliver of gold!

"Holy cow!"

"He just spun it once, and directly got a Personal Disciple qualification!"

"This is outrageous! I must go up and spin it today!"

"Brothers! Charge! We must get a Personal Disciple qualification today!"

Everyone eagerly rubbed their hands, determined to spin themselves a Personal Disciple qualification!

"Everyone! The wheel will record everyone's Divine Soul, and you can only spin it once in your lifetime!"

"Please also rest assured that the wheel will only record your Divine Soul and will not have any impact on you!"

"Now, please line up, one by one!"

Immediately, the residents of Biluo City began to rush towards the nearest spinning wheel.

"Wahahahaha! Golden legend! I'm coming! Personal Disciple! I'm coming!"

"Nonsense! The Personal Disciple must be mine!"

"Get lost! How can someone who chokes on cold water hope to hit Personal Disciple?"

Everyone wanted to be the first to spin, hoping to get a golden outcome!

Seeing the chaotic scene, Long Aotian had to exert his power again!

"Everyone, get down!"

With an angry roar, coupled with the sudden eruption of his Great Sage Realm aura, all the residents of Biluo City were instantly pressed to the ground by Long Aotian!

"Hmph! Let's see who dares to mess around now? I'll cripple him!"

This move was indeed effective!

The subsequent process was very orderly!

But the Imperial Guard from the Great Xia Empire, who was at the very back, was grimacing inwardly!

"It's you who should put all your thoughts into Cultivation."

Chu Xian laughed heartily, unaware that Yu Jian was serious!

Changing his mind, Chu Xian then said:

From bottom to top, a pair of jade feet and fair, long legs were revealed, and her waterfall-like long hair naturally fell, covering her perfectly curved back.

20: Chu Xian spread the Dao~

My Heart Demon, my foot!

Does this Emperor need you to tell her how to Cultivate?

I think you just want to peek at this Emperor!

You old pervert!

Hmph!

This child's talent is a bit too poor... "Yes, Revered Master!"

In my previous life, this Emperor only took fifty years to become a Saint, and reached the Half-Emperor Realm by the age of one hundred!

Revered Master! You still need to practice!

That's it? The sword stance is loose and weak, and the Moves are full of flaws!

Then you don't know the secrets of this Emperor's previous life Sect!

Those could only be described as world-destroying!

Yours, it's far from that!

Why?

Simply because this was an Emperor-Grade Sword Technique from the Myriad Scripture Pavilion of the Lingxiao Sect!

Oh my, oh my, oh my!

21: A golden legend! A spirit stone mine!

23: The prince's purpose! The Formless Spirit Ruins!

A tentacle sliced across, and Yu Jian’s arm instantly tore open, bleeding profusely!

“Yu Jian!”

Tang Liu let out a cold shout, his fish trident wrapped in dark golden light, stabbing towards the black mist, but it was like a mud ox entering the sea!

Xia Jie’s Imperial Dragon Qi was also devoured by the black mist, and his face changed dramatically:

“This demonic thing can devour spiritual power!”

Within the black mist, a pair of crimson eyes slowly opened, and a hoarse voice echoed:

“Lingxiao Sect… all deserve to die!”

The black mist surged, pressing down on the three like a towering tidal wave!

“Boom—”

Yu Jian roared, his fist condensing all his spiritual power, and he slammed it violently towards the center of the black mist.

However, as soon as the force of the punch touched the black mist, it was completely devoured!

“Be careful!”

Tang Liu swept his fish trident horizontally, dark golden patterns flickering wildly on his arm, and a chop severed the tentacle attacking Xia Jie.

But the broken tentacle, after falling to the ground, turned into black water and instantly merged back into the mist!

“This demonic thing can’t be killed?!”

Cold sweat seeped from Xia Jie’s forehead, and golden Dragon Qi continuously blasted out from his hand, but with little effect.

Suddenly, Yu Jian’s pupils constricted: “Look at those tentacles!”

On each writhing black mist tentacle, a distorted human face vaguely appeared!

There were the ferocious faces of the Li Family members, the pained expressions of Fentian Valley Disciples, and even the three royal Family members who had just perished!

Of course, there were also many faces the three didn’t recognize.

“It… it devoured everyone…?”

Xia Jie’s voice trembled, “I’m afraid there are fewer than twenty living people left in the Formless Spirit Ruins now…”

“Screech!”

A tentacle suddenly pierced through Tang Liu’s defense, tearing a bloody gash on his shoulder.

As soon as the dark golden blood seeped out, it was greedily absorbed by the black mist!

“Hahahaha!”

Li Jing’s crazed voice came from within the black mist, “Do you see? This is the consequence of going against the Li Family!”

“Li Jing?!” Yu Jian roared, “Are you f*cking crazy?!”

His answer was three rapidly growing tentacles, each carrying the pressure of the Peak of the Spirit Spring Realm!

Xia Jie hurriedly brought out the royal Family’s secret Magic Treasure, the “Nine Dragon Seal.” Nine golden Dragon illusory figures swirled out, temporarily forcing back the black mist.

But cracks immediately appeared on the Magic Treasure’s surface, clearly indicating it wouldn’t last much longer.

“If this continues, we’ll surely die…” Tang Liu wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth, the dark golden patterns already spreading to his neck, “We must find the core!”

Although Yu Jian and the two others had not found Li Jing’s weakness, it was by no means easy for Li Jing to achieve a quick victory!

Just as the battle entered a white-hot stage, the black mist suddenly churned violently.

The devoured human faces actually began to tear at each other, emitting piercing wails!

“No… don’t…”

Li Jing’s voice suddenly became terrified, “Senior! You promised to let me keep my consciousness!”

An even older, hoarse voice sounded: “Useless… can’t even take down three juniors…”

In the center of the black mist, a pulsating black heart gradually appeared.

Each beat made the entire space tremble!

“Now!” Tang Liu’s eyes shone brightly.

The three simultaneously unleashed their strongest attack—

Yu Jian’s fists ignited with blood-red flames, Tang Liu’s fish trident transformed into dark golden lightning, and Xia Jie bit his tongue, spitting out a drop of vital blood to activate the Nine Dragon Seal!

“Bang—!”

The shockwave of the explosion destroyed all trees within a hundred zhang radius!

When the smoke and dust cleared, Tang Liu’s fish trident was… impaled in that black heart!

“Ah ah ah!”

The black mist let out a heart-wrenching scream and began to rapidly contract.

But before the three could even catch their breath, they heard a “crack”!

Liu Zi’s fish trident… broke!



42: Emerging from the Spirit Ruins

"Crack!"

The crisp sound of the trident breaking in Tang Liu's ears was like a clap of thunder.

He stared blankly at the weapon his father had obtained before his death, the dark golden patterns instantly spread from his neck to his entire face, and his pupils contracted to the size of pinpricks.

"Trident..."

"It broke."

The moment the last word fell, the ground within a radius of ten zhang centered on Tang Liu suddenly sank three feet!

The violent Blood Qi formed a visible dark red Qi wave, shattering all the surrounding rubble into dust.

"Li Jing" had just revealed a mocking smile when his vision suddenly blurred—

"Bang!"

Tang Liu's fist had already smashed heavily into his face, black blood mixed with a few teeth sprayed out.

The force of this punch directly created a sonic boom cloud!

The ensuing battle completely defied logic.

Tang Liu completely abandoned defense, each punch carrying the power to tear the sky and rip the earth.

He tore off the incoming black mist tentacles with his bare hands, and the dark golden blood made a "hissing" sound like a hot iron entering water when it touched the black mist.

"Boom!"

An elbow strike slammed "Li Jing" into the rock wall, and Tang Liu followed up with a knee strike to his abdomen, causing the entire cliff to collapse under this single blow!

Xia Jie watched dumbfounded!

Is this still Tang Liu?!

Never provoke Tang Liu!

The consequences are severe!

"Li Jing" was also secretly shocked internally:

What's wrong with this kid?! Can the War God Bloodline be this strong?!

Just as Tang Liu was clutching "Li Jing's" throat and lifting him, an unexpected change occurred.

"Li Jing's" mouth curved into a strange smile.

Black mist suddenly drilled into Tang Liu's body from his seven orifices, and the dark golden patterns were instantly stained with black.

Tang Liu grunted, his movements momentarily freezing.

"Hehehe! Got you."

"Li Jing" grinned, his five fingers like knives stabbing towards Tang Liu's heart—

"Chirp!"

The sound of a sharp blade entering flesh rang out, but it was Yu Jian who was injured.

He pushed Tang Liu away at the last moment, but was himself pierced through the shoulder.

"Ant!"

"Li Jing" grinned, just about to finish off Yu Jian when—

Suddenly, a golden light shot up from afar, piercing through the clouds!

"Li Jing's" heart trembled violently:

The aura of the Emperor's Path?! No... this is... the Immortal Path!

Hahahaha! Heaven helps me!

The War God Bloodline actually appeared at the same time as the Immortal Path!

They are all mine!

He violently shook off the crowd, greedily looking in the direction of the golden light.

At this moment, in his eyes, Tang Liu's Bloodline could be set aside for a while; if he missed the formation of the Immortal Path, he wouldn't get it!

So, he unhesitatingly abandoned Tang Liu, transforming into a black light and rushing straight towards the source of the golden light!

Yu Jian and the other two also gained a moment to breathe.

"Senior Brother!"

The three Lingxiao Sect Disciples stumbled over, taking advantage of the opportunity, their nine higanbana petals in their hands emitting a dreamy glow.

The eldest Disciple's lips trembled: "Senior Brother, higanbana petals! Nine of them!"

Yu Jian clutched the wound on his shoulder and took a Medicinal Pill for healing.

Seeing the three hand him petals, he couldn't help but be stunned: "What time is it, why are you giving me petals?"

At that time, Yu Jian was seeing Emperor Xia off when Chu Xian gave the order to the Disciples, so he was not present and didn't know the purpose of these higanbana petals.

Tang Liu, however, his eyes lit up; he had kept Chu Xian's instructions in mind.

He took the petals and put them into his nascent void ring.

"Let's go! Lin Xuan is still over there!"

Xia Jie frowned, but said nothing more, following their footsteps.

As they ran for a short distance, the entire ground suddenly began to twist!

The phase refresh began again!

This phase refresh carried a destructive power, mountains collapsed, rivers flowed backward, as if the entire world was collapsing towards the center!

A hundred-zhang boulder swept over their heads, disappearing into the horizon in a blink of an eye.

"Not good!" Xia Jie's face changed drastically, "Is the Formless Spirit Ruins about to close?"

"Why is it so fast this time?"

The few of them ran desperately, the ground behind them shattering inch by inch.

And ahead, the golden light and black mist had already begun to collide wildly!

Amidst the collision of golden light and black mist, Lin Xuan slowly opened her eyes.

Her pupils had turned light gold, and a faint Immortal aura lingered around her, every move she made caused the spiritual energy of heaven and earth to spontaneously gather.

"Li Jing" in the black mist stared at her, the greed in his eyes almost materializing:

"Immortal Bone?! You actually have an Immortal Bone within you?!"

"No wonder the Immortal Ascension path appeared!"

"I only need to dig out your Immortal Bone to gain the qualification for Immortal Ascension!"

Lin Xuan did not answer, she just raised her hand, and a golden Sword Qi condensed out of thin air, slashing towards "Li Jing"!

Only Er Bai on the side barked excitedly with his tongue out:

"Woof!"

The golden Sword Qi slammed into the black mist!

"Boom—!"

Both retreated several steps simultaneously, evenly matched!

"Li Jing" gave a sinister laugh, then suddenly flashed, abandoning Lin Xuan and rushing straight towards Xiao Daidai and the others behind her!

"Be careful!" Lin Xuan's face changed slightly, and she hastily flashed to intercept.

"Chirp!"

A black mist blade sliced across her arm, drawing a string of golden blood beads.

Although "Li Jing's" sneak attack was blocked, Lin Xuan was slightly injured as a result, and her aura paused momentarily.

Seizing this momentary flaw, "Li Jing's" offensive suddenly became ferocious!

The black mist transformed into countless sharp blades, attacking Lin Xuan from all directions.

Although she desperately resisted, her movements were no longer as fluid as before, and she gradually fell into a disadvantage.

"Senior Sister!"

Xiao Daidai anxiously tried to help, but was thrown back by the violent spiritual energy aftermath.

Just at this critical moment—

"Boom!"

A figure descended from the sky, Yu Jian bathed in blood, his fist wind like a Dragon, forcefully blasting open a patch of black mist!

"Junior Sister, we're here!"

Tang Liu and Xia Jie followed closely, the three forming a triangle to protect Lin Xuan in the center.

However, before they could regroup, the entire world suddenly began to tremble!

Countless starlight rose from the ground, mountains, rivers, plants... everything was gradually becoming ethereal.

"The Formless Spirit Ruins is closing!" Xia Jie exclaimed.

"Li Jing's" face was ecstatic, "Haha! Hahahaha! I, finally, am leaving this cursed place!"

"Little devils, we'll meet outside!"

Before he finished speaking, his body also began to dissipate into black mist.

Everyone could only watch helplessly as the black mist dissipated, unable to do anything.

Xia Jie quickly gathered everyone together, preparing to leave the Formless Spirit Ruins.

The surrounding world gradually turned into starlight.

A dazzling white light flashed, and when everyone opened their eyes again, they were standing in the desert at the entrance of the Formless Spirit Ruins.

What greeted Yu Jian and the others was a human purgatory!

The entrance to the Formless Spirit Ruins, which should have been bustling with people, had now become a bloody hell!

The yellow sand was soaked with blood, and severed limbs were scattered everywhere.

The chests of numerous corpses wearing various faction uniforms seemed to have been pierced by something.

Three hundred zhang away, that familiar black mist was being furiously beaten by a young man!

"Damn it! Where are my Lingxiao Sect Disciples? Why did you, this ghostly thing, come out instead?"

The Lingxiao Sect members exclaimed in unison:

"Revered Master!"

"Sect Master!"



43: Chu Xian, the curious little guy!

"Boom!"

Chu Xian slapped the black mist, sending it tumbling dozens of feet. A shrill scream from "Li Jing" echoed from within the black mist.

"Damn it! Where are my Lingxiao Sect Disciples? How did a ghostly thing like you appear instead?"

Chu Xian cursed, then kicked it again.

"Li Jing" in the black mist was on the verge of tears!

After finally escaping the Formless Spirit Ruins, where he had been imprisoned for a thousand years, he had just emerged and eaten a few human hearts when he ran into a Great Sage Realm expert!

His cultivation was directly sealed by this Great Sage, and he was being beaten down by a mere Peak of the Spirit Spring Realm Cultivator!

Was there any justice in this?!

"Revered Master!"

"Sect Master!"

The joyful shouts of the Lingxiao Sect Disciples came. Chu Xian turned his head and saw that all his Disciples were standing not far away, and he breathed a sigh of relief.

"Why didn't you say so earlier!"

Chu Xian backhanded the black mist again. "You deserve this beating!"

After understanding the situation from the Disciples, everyone began to recount "Li Jing's" evil deeds in the Formless Spirit Ruins.

Devouring Cultivators, ambushing Lingxiao Sect Disciples, attempting to seize Tang Liu's Bloodline and Immortal Bone... Chu Xian listened, then impatiently waved his hand: "Crush it."

Long Aotian chuckled, grabbed with his large hand, and directly crushed the black mist!

"Bang!"

The black mist exploded, turning into wisps of black smoke and dissipating.

Just as everyone was discussing matters in the Formless Spirit Ruins, Xia Yanqiu, the Emperor of the Great Xia Empire, arrived, treading on air.

He nodded slightly to Chu Xian, his gaze subtly sweeping over Yu Jian.

Chu Xian took Er Bai from Yu Jian's arms and watched as Er Bai's front paws incessantly rubbed his small nose, laughing:

"Alright, let's return to Piaomiao City first. This place has too strong a smell of blood, Er Bai isn't used to it anymore."

Everyone said no more and quickly returned to Piaomiao City.

As soon as they left, a black-robed figure silently appeared in the desert.

He squatted down, his fingers lightly touching the sand. A moment later, he picked up a wisp of almost imperceptible black mist, his expression incredibly respectful:

"Welcome, Old Ancestor, to your freedom!"

Immediately, the black-robed figure transformed into black smoke and dissipated.

On the top floor of Zui Xian Lou, gauze curtains gently swayed.

A peerlessly beautiful woman lay quietly on a jade couch, her skin like snow, her features like a painting, yet she seemed to have been asleep for a thousand years, utterly devoid of Life Qi.

This woman was the Great Xia Princess, Xia Zhu.

Chu Xian turned to look at all his Disciples:

"Have the higanbana petals been found?"

Tang Liu immediately took out nine crystal-clear petals.

He then explained to Chu Xian that the three Disciples had found them.

Chu Xian looked at the three with satisfaction, and they immediately scratched their heads in embarrassment.

Chu Xian said: "Upon returning to the Sect, the three of you may soak in the bloodline immortal pool for ten days!"

The three Disciples were overjoyed, repeatedly expressing their thanks:

"This Disciple thanks Sect Master!"

"Thank you, Sect Master!"

Afterward, Chu Xian sent everyone out, leaving only Yu Jian and Xia Yanqiu.

"Yu Jian."

Chu Xian was uncharacteristically serious, pointing to the woman on the jade couch and saying:

"This is the Great Xia Princess, Xia Zhu."

"She possesses the Ancient Yellow Springs Sacred Body. Before its Awakening, she will remain in a deep sleep. To awaken her, higanbana petals are needed..."

He paused, then added meaningfully: "...and intercourse with a Pure Yang Body."

Yu Jian nodded thoughtfully: "These conditions are quite strict. A Pure Yang Body is not easy to find."

Chu Xian immediately rubbed his forehead: "You're good in every way, kid, except your thought process is different from everyone else's!"

The one lying there is called Xia Zhu, you'll be called Silly Spring from now on!

"You possess the Great Grade Celestial Body! An absolute Pure Yang Body!"

Only then did Yu Jian react!

His eyes widened instantly: "Wait! Revered Master, you mean..."

Xia Yanqiu suddenly bowed respectfully: "Please, Young Friend Yu, save my daughter's life!"

Yu Jian's face instantly flushed red. He retreated several steps, waving his hand, "Your Majesty, this junior respectfully declines!"

Chu Xian shouted: "You good-for-nothing!"

"Revered Master, this... this won't do! This Disciple is devoted to the Dao, how can I..."

Chu Xian glared: "Why won't it do? The princess is as beautiful as a flower, and she possesses an Ancient Saint Body. Is she not worthy of you?"

Yu Jian was so anxious that sweat beaded on his forehead: "It's not a matter of worthiness! This Disciple... this Disciple..."

He stammered for a long time, then suddenly gritted his teeth: "This Disciple's life was saved by Revered Master. I only wish to serve Revered Master's side and do not wish to be distracted by other matters!"

Chu Xian was stunned, then his eyes reddened slightly.

His Disciple's heart was pure; he was refusing such a good thing because he wanted to take care of him.

He remembered that time he teased him, and he said he wouldn't marry a Dao Companion. Chu Xian had thought he said it to make him happy.

"Silly boy..."

Chu Xian rubbed Yu Jian's head, his voice uncharacteristically gentle: "Your Revered Master isn't so old that he needs you to take care of him."

"But..."

"Listen to me."

Chu Xian said seriously: "If the Ancient Yellow Springs Sacred Body is not awakened in time, Xia Zhu will surely die within a year. Can you bear to see such a beautiful girl die in vain?"

Xia Yanqiu hurriedly stepped forward and said:

"Young friend! I beg you! I only hope that Zhu'er can live..."

"After this matter, no matter the outcome, my Great Xia will forever be a subordinate of the Lingxiao Sect!"

Yu Jian looked at Chu Xian, then at the humble Xia Yanqiu, and finally sighed:

"Alright..."

Chu Xian immediately beamed, and quickly explained the Awakening method in detail:

"Remember, after taking the higanbana petals, you need to use your Pure Yang Blood as a guide, and then... ahem..."

Chu Xian's old face flushed. He quickly pulled Xia Yanqiu and exited the room.

"Bang!"

The moment the door closed, Chu Xian's faint instructions could be heard:

"My Disciple, be gentle..."

The main hall on the eighth floor of Zui Xian Lou.

Everyone was discussing heatedly.

The Zui Xian Lou shopkeeper recounted everything that had happened during the time everyone was in the Formless Spirit Ruins.

It turned out that everyone only felt like a day or two had passed in the Formless Spirit Ruins, but a whole month had passed in the outside world!

During the opening period of the Formless Spirit Ruins, because Long Aotian had eaten most of the Li family's Azure Luan Guards, the Great Xia Imperial Family directly used thunderous means to destroy the Li family, and all of the Li family's crimes were listed one by one.

At the same time, the high-ranking officials of ten great Sects, including Piaomiao Sect and Fentian Valley, were all executed by the Great Xia Imperial Family!

With the Li family's Azure Luan Guards no longer restraining them, the Northern Border Demon Race was directly pursued for ten thousand li by the Willow Leaf Legion and was on the verge of extinction.

This series of news left the Lingxiao Sect Disciples and the Imperial Family members all with wide-open mouths.

At this moment, Chu Xian and Xia Yanqiu happened to walk down from upstairs.

Everyone stood up and bowed.

Chu Xian waved his hand and said: "Alright, everyone sit down. How was this training? What did you all encounter inside?"

So everyone began to talk about their various experiences in the Formless Spirit Ruins.

Chu Xian listened intently, nodding frequently.

However, everyone seemed to have agreed beforehand, as not a single person mentioned Lin Xuan's past life memories!

Finally, when everyone spoke of Lin Xuan's great battle with the black mist and how Lin Xuan was injured by it.

This former Half-Emperor suddenly stood up, angrily saying:

"Bah! If this were my past life, a mouthful of spit would spray..."

At this point, Lin Xuan instantly froze!

Oh no! Did I say the wrong thing?

How did I reveal my most important secret?

Chu Xian's eyes widened, looking at Lin Xuan in confusion.

Just then, Tang Liu spoke: "Revered Master, she... died before."

Chu Xian instantly transformed into a curious baby!

"Oh? Liu'er, quickly tell your Revered Master! What happened?"

As he spoke, he somehow pulled out a large handful of fragrant melon seeds from somewhere!



44: Lin Xuan returns home! Xia Zhu wakes up.

Drunken Immortal Tower top floor.

Candlelight flickered, Yu Jian stood before the jade couch, his fingertips trembling slightly.

On the couch, the young girl's snow-white skin glowed with a faint golden radiance, her dark hair cascaded like a waterfall.

He took a deep breath, placed nine higanbana petals into his mouth, and gently leaned down.

The higanbana petals in Yu Jian's mouth transformed into nine drops of clear liquid, which dripped into Xia Zhu's mouth.

Suddenly, a golden pattern flashed between the young girl's eyebrows, and her sleeping body seemed to be awakened by a supremely Yang Qi!

Although Xia Zhu's eyes were tightly closed, her jade fingers unconsciously untied Yu Jian's sash... On the eighth floor of Drunken Immortal Tower, the lights were bright and the aroma of wine permeated the air.

After listening to everyone recount Lin Xuan's past life, Chu Xian remained silent for a moment, then suddenly slammed the table, making the wine cups clatter.

"Xuan'er!"

Chu Xian strode to Lin Xuan's front, placing his palm gently on her shoulder.

"We couldn't help you with your past, but in this life, you have joined my Lingxiao Sect. Your Revered Master will surely help you reach the peak again and avenge that life-taking grudge!"

Lin Xuan was slightly startled, a ripple of emotion flashing in her eyes.

"No seat!"

Little Dumb-Dumb leaned over, still clutching half a string of candied hawthorns, "Senior Sister, if anyone dares to bully you, I, Little Dumb-Dumb, will be the first to object!"

"Count me in."

Tang Liu's voice was low but incredibly firm, "People of the Lingxiao Sect are not to be trifled with."

Following that, all the Inner Sect Disciples and Outer Sect Disciples of the Lingxiao Sect expressed their resolve, vowing to follow Lin Xuan in this life and avenge the grudges of her past life!

Lin Xuan looked at the sincere faces before her, and the cold barrier brought by her past life's memories melted away like spring snow at this moment.

Although her past life had reached the Half-Emperor Realm, she was now merely a Personal Disciple of the Lingxiao Sect.

She should cherish the present!

Lin Xuan took a deep breath, then suddenly knelt on one knee:

"Disciple Lin Xuan, thanks Revered Master, thanks fellow Disciples."

"Get up, get up!"

Chu Xian pulled her up, and casually stuffed a jar of strong wine into her arms.

"My Disciple, how about we drink until we're drunk tonight?"

Wine jars clinked, and fine wine splashed.

Amidst the cheers of the Disciples, Lin Xuan tilted her head back and drank heartily, the spicy liquor sliding down her throat.

Yet, for the first time, she tasted the flavor of "home."

Outside the window, the Bright Moon hung in the sky, making the golden pattern between her eyebrows shine brightly.

This Reincarnation Half-Emperor finally completely let down her guard at this moment, integrating into this noisy and warm world.

Just as everyone was raising their cups and drinking merrily, a dull "thud" suddenly came from above, causing even the tables on the eighth floor to shake slightly!

"Hiss--"

Little Dumb-Dumb bit into her candied hawthorn, her eyes wide, "Senior Brother Yu... is he tearing down the building?"

An Inner Sect Disciple, slightly tipsy at this point, joked:

"Judging by that noise, it's probably even more intense than tearing down the building! Hahaha!"

Everyone stifled their laughter, winking at each other.

Lin Xuan lightly coughed, pretending to lower her head to drink, but her earlobes were slightly red.

Not long after, footsteps echoed from the stairwell.

Yu Jian, covering half of his flushed face, with faint scratch marks visible on his neck, walked down with unsteady steps.

"Ma... Revered Master... Your Majesty..."

Yu Jian's voice was clearly weak, and his gaze drifted.

Chu Xian glared at Yu Jian, saying irritably, "Still calling him Your Majesty?"

Yu Jian jolted, then quickly cupped his hands to Xia Yanqiu:

"Your Majesty, rest assured, I, Yu Jian, will never fail Xia Zhu in this life!"

Xia Yanqiu nodded in satisfaction:

"From now on, visit the Imperial Palace more often with Zhu'er."

"Yes... Father-in-law..."

Chu Xian chuckled lightly and announced:

"From today onwards, Xia Zhu is the fourth Personal Disciple of my Lingxiao Sect!"

Yu Jian's eyes instantly widened!

Hmm?

How do I feel like Revered Master had already planned to take Xia Zhu as a Disciple?

Was I tricked by Revered Master?

Everyone burst into laughter, surrounding Yu Jian and teasing him.

"Senior Brother, why is your face swollen? Scratched by Second White?"

"That's not right! Second White has been sleeping all along and didn't go upstairs!"

"Tsk tsk, these scratch marks... fierce battle!"

"..."

Yu Jian angrily waved everyone away, but couldn't help but secretly glance at the stairs leading to the top floor... In the private room on the eighth floor of Drunken Immortal Tower, Chu Xian casually flipped through the map of the Great Xia Imperial Family territory presented by Xia Yanqiu.

Xia Yanqiu, in turn, reported to Chu Xian one by one.

"On the first day of each month, the Imperial Family will send seventy percent of the mine's income to the Lingxiao Holy Land."

Xia Yanqiu's fingertips traced several vermilion markings on the map.

Chu Xian nodded lightly; he had handled many such Sect affiliated matters when he was in the Yunxiao Sect.

"In exchange, my Lingxiao Sect will ensure the peace of Great Xia and the Great Xia Imperial Family."

"But there is one thing, the Great Xia Imperial Family must not commit evil in the name of my Lingxiao Sect."

Xia Yanqiu's face showed joy; this time, Great Xia and the Lingxiao Sect were completely bound together!

"Holy Revered Master, rest assured, this will never happen!"

Chu Xian continued, "How are the preparations for recruiting Disciples coming along?"

"I have already drafted an imperial decree, and from today onwards, Holy Land Disciple recruitment points will be set up throughout Great Xia."

"Once each recruitment point gathers a hundred people, the Imperial Family will send them to the Holy Land via Daozhou."

Chu Xian nodded in satisfaction.

"Long Aotian has already returned to the Sect to retrieve the Disciple recruitment roulette, and is expected to return in five days."

Xia Yanqiu's body couldn't help but tremble!

It would have been better not to mention this; once it was mentioned, Xia Yanqiu felt that the Spirit Stones given by the Imperial Family to the Lingxiao Sect were a bit of a waste...

Recruiting Disciples entirely depended on luck!

He had never seen such an outrageous method of Disciple recruitment in his life!

...The next day.

When Xia Zhu finally awoke from her slumber, her originally ink-black eyes had turned a deep dark gold, and a faint Yellow Springs Qi lingered around her.

"Father..." she softly called, her voice still hoarse from just waking up.

Xia Yanqiu quickly stepped forward, tightly grasping his daughter's hand, his eyes slightly red:

"Zhu'er, you're finally awake!"

Before he finished speaking, Xia Zhu's gaze bypassed Xia Yanqiu and fell directly on Yu Jian at the door.

The young girl's pale cheeks suddenly flushed, and her fingertips unconsciously tightened the brocade quilt.

Xia Yanqiu, however, paid no attention, gripping Xia Zhu's hand with even greater excitement.

"Cough!"

Chu Xian coughed heavily, and Xia Yanqiu immediately reacted.

"Zhu'er, quickly come and greet your Revered Master. Your awakening is entirely thanks to your Revered Master."

Xia Zhu nodded lightly, and was about to get out of bed to bow, but Chu Xian quickly stopped her,

"Zhu'er has just woken up, so let's skip the extra courtesies!"

Then he grinned and said, "From now on, you are a Personal Disciple of the Lingxiao Sect!"

"Your Revered Master has the "Nine Revolutions Yellow Springs Art" here, Zhu'er must Cultivate starting today!"

Xia Yanqiu's pupils instantly contracted!

Eh?

Didn't you say you didn't have it?

Didn't you also say it had to be used with some kind of tower?

Now you're just taking it out directly?

Was I tricked?

Chu Xian glanced at Xia Yanqiu, gently blinking, as if to say:

How's that?

Am I generous to my daughter-in-law and Disciple?

Xia Yanqiu's gaze shifted slightly, and he thought to himself:

Sigh! Let him trick me! My daughter being awake is more important than anything else!

Little Dumb-Dumb ran smiling to Xia Zhu, stuffing her hands full of Spirit Fruits.

She whispered, "Senior Sister, eat quickly, Senior Brother Yu specially picked these for you!"

Amidst everyone's laughter, Yu Jian's ears turned crimson, and he stammered, "I, I just happened to be passing by..."

Xia Zhu lowered her head and pursed her lips: "Thank you... Senior Brother."

Subsequently, everyone discreetly and tactfully exited the top floor, leaving space for Yu Jian and Xia Zhu.



45: Yun Feiyang's Choice

In the tavern opposite the Zui Xian Lou, Xia Jie unconsciously crushed the jade cup in his hand.

A young eunuch beside him softly reminded, "Your Highness, it's time to return to the palace."

Xia Jie abruptly stood up and left with a flick of his sleeve.

Meanwhile, a wisp of black mist quietly seeped into his hair... A hundred miles north of Piaomiao City, within the Piaomiao Sect.

Yun Feiyang finally returned from the Formless Spirit Ruins.

While in the Formless Spirit Ruins, Yun Feiyang had retreated directly after battling Yu Jian, and then encountered an attack from Spirit Beasts, leading two Piaomiao Sect Disciples in a fierce fight against them.

Due to being a bit far from the central area, he did not encounter the demonized Li Jing, thus escaping a calamity.

Later, when the Formless Spirit Ruins closed, only a pile of mangled limbs remained in the desert when the three of them emerged.

The three didn't take it to heart; when had the Formless Spirit Ruins ever closed without rivers of blood?

But when the three entered the Piaomiao Sect, they sensed something was amiss!

The entire Piaomiao Sect was shrouded in Death Qi, and the Disciples were all hurrying with their belongings.

Yun Feiyang stopped one person and asked sharply:
