Another three whips went down, and although Da Huang howled in pain, it was clear that Da Huang's Aura was different!

"Boom!"

A muffled sound came from within Da Huang's body!

"Spirit Spring Realm First Layer! Da Huang, you can do it!"

Chu Xian immediately beamed with joy!

"Good stuff! To put it bluntly, it's pure good stuff!"

Before Da Huang could react, Chu Xian snatched the long gown from Da Huang's body and scurried down Main Peak!

Only Da Huang and Second White were left bewildered on Main Peak!

Who am I?

Where am I?

How did I Breakthrough?

Inside Lingxiao Sect.

Chu Xian ran wildly all the way to the Inner Sect Disciple area.

"You all! Who wants to be whipped by This Sect Master?"

The numerous Inner Sect Disciples were stunned!

What was wrong with the Sect Master?

He was holding a small whip in one hand and a piece of clothing in the other, and he was asking who wanted to be whipped?

Seeing no one respond, Chu Xian went directly to a Female Disciple.

"It's you!"

As he spoke, he directly draped the long gown over the Female Disciple's body.

The next second, a bunny girl instantly blinded the dog eyes of all the male Disciples!

Then Chu Xian swung his small leather whip!

"Crack!"

The Female Disciple's face turned red!

"Sect... Sect Master..."

"Disciple..."

"Stop talking nonsense! Feel the spiritual power in your body carefully!"



49: Those who want to be whipped, line up, one by one!

At this moment, Chu Xian's voice boomed in the Female Disciple's mind like a great bell.

Although the Female Disciple's face was flushed, she quickly guided the Spiritual Qi within her body according to Chu Xian's instructions.

Her consciousness sinking into her body, the Female Disciple suddenly discovered that a massive surge of Spiritual Qi was pouring into her back where the Sect Master had whipped her!

Her entire back felt tingly and numb, and the Female Disciple's body couldn't help but tremble.

Even the two rabbit ears on her head swayed twice.

"Sect Master... This Disciple... seems to be... on the verge of a Breakthrough!"

Upon hearing that she was about to have a Breakthrough, Chu Xian immediately struck while the iron was hot!

"Whap! Whap! Whap!"

Chu Xian's face was expressionless as he whipped the Female Disciple with the small leather whip.

After three more lashes, the Female Disciple felt her Cultivation path suddenly become clear.

Her spirit became incredibly lucid!

Her understanding of Cultivation Technique instantly seemed to rise several levels!

The Female Disciple quickly sat cross-legged, entering an Epiphany in an instant!

Epiphany is a Realm countless Cultivators dream of.

According to the "Xuanxiao Continent Cultivation Guide," when a Cultivator enters an Epiphany, Breakthroughs are as easy as drinking water, and their cultivation can advance a thousand miles in a day!

However, an Epiphany is something that cannot be sought, only encountered.

Even with high talent, an Epiphany will not occur unless under specific circumstances.

Seeing that the Female Disciple had entered a state of Epiphany, Chu Xian nodded with satisfaction, quietly standing guard nearby and ordering everyone not to disturb her.

Everyone stared intently at the rabbit-girl-dressed Female Disciple, not only wanting to see the wonders of the Epiphany Realm but also to observe the interesting attire...

Within a few breaths, the Female Disciple had already broken through from the first level of the Meridian Connecting Realm to the third level of the Meridian Connecting Realm!

And her cultivation continued to grow!

At this, all the Disciples became restless!

Everyone's starting points were originally similar, but with This Sect Master's sudden action, this Female Disciple's cultivation had already taken a commanding lead. Who could tolerate that?

Because the Female Disciple's innate talent wasn't too high, the Epiphany Realm only lasted for less than half a minute before ending.

But in that half-minute, this Female Disciple directly broke through from the first level of the Meridian Connecting Realm to the seventh level of the Meridian Connecting Realm!

The combination of whip of god and the dream transformation was simply terrifying!

The moment the Female Disciple opened her eyes, before she could even thank Chu Xian, she saw the surrounding Inner Sect Disciples yelling and rushing towards Chu Xian!

"Sect Master! Please whip me, Sect Master!"

"Sect Master! This Disciple has a better physique; wearing that attire will surely make me break through faster! Sect Master, you must choose me!"

"Sect Master! ..."

The scene was incredibly chaotic!

Chu Xian looked at so many people without order and said helplessly:

"Those who want to be whipped, line up! One by one!"

The Inner Sect Disciples could only line up obediently according to their order of joining the Sect.

Currently, Lingxiao Sect has over thirty Inner Sect Disciples, and whipping them one by one wouldn't take too long.

Since Chu Xian didn't need to Cultivate anyway, he simply had someone bring him a chair and sat there, urging them one by one.

Inner Sect Disciple residence:

"Whap!"

"Ah!"

"Comfortable! Sect Master, harder! This Disciple can still take it!"

"Whap! Whap! Whap!"

The Epiphany Realm appeared again!

"Next!"

"Whap! Whap!"

...For an entire day, Chu Xian stayed at the Inner Sect Disciple's residence, watching everyone's cultivation improve significantly, before contentedly leaving with his small leather whip and long gown.

After returning to the Main Peak, Chu Xian was leisurely sipping tea on his recliner when he saw Lin Xuan excitedly walk over.

"Revered Master! Why do you give the good stuff to the Inner Sect first, but not to us Direct Disciples?"

Chu Xian was stunned!

"Hmm? You already know this news?"

"I thought you guys didn't need this, but since you're here, This Revered Master will let you taste the feeling of being urged."

Lin Xuan gave Chu Xian a cold glance.

This is more like it!

Lin Xuan had long yearned for the Epiphany Realm!

Even as a Half-Emperor expert in her previous life, she had never experienced even a single Epiphany!

If she had experienced an Epiphany in her previous life, she would probably have already achieved the Emperor position!

An Epiphany not only allows a Cultivator to quickly improve their cultivation in a short time but also makes their future Dao path smoother.

"Xuan'er, are you ready? This Revered Master is coming!"

Lin Xuan, with her back to Chu Xian, didn't answer, only nodded nervously.

Lin Xuan was simply too eager for the Epiphany Realm, to the point where her heart was in her throat!

After draping the long gown over Lin Xuan, a white light flashed!

Chu Xian was utterly dumbfounded!

Black stockings, long legs, a hip-hugging mini-skirt... coupled with Lin Xuan's proud figure and peerless beauty... she was simply the bane of all men!

Lin Xuan was also stunned when she saw the attire on her body!

"Revered Master!"

"What do you mean by this!"

Clearly, besides shyness, Lin Xuan also felt some annoyance.

She thought: This old Revered Master is indeed a lecherous old man!

"Uh..."

"Disciple, This Revered Master will begin urging you now!"

"Whap!"

The whip of god came down, and with just one lash, Lin Xuan had already entered the Epiphany Realm!

"Xuan'er is indeed powerful; those Inner Sect Disciples needed at least three whips to enter an Epiphany."

Chu Xian looked at Lin Xuan, who had entered the Epiphany Realm, and nodded slightly.

But what surprised Chu Xian was that Lin Xuan's cultivation had not improved; she was still at the Peak of the Spirit Spring Realm.

"Huh? Strange..."

Chu Xian waited for a while and found no signs of Lin Xuan waking up.

"It seems the duration of the Epiphany Realm is indeed related to one's innate talent. Xuan'er has been in an Epiphany for two minutes already!"

So Chu Xian arranged for Long Aotian to guard Lin Xuan, not allowing anyone to disturb her.

He, on the other hand, ran off to the Lingxiao Sect's Main Hall to ponder.

"Sigh, urging Disciples like this every day isn't a long-term solution; I don't even have time for myself anymore!"

Chu Xian raised an eyebrow, "Red Feather, Up and Down!"

The two Elders instantly appeared.

"Sect Master!"

Chu Xian said, "I'm giving you two a task."

"Set up a mission hall within the Sect, assign missions to Disciples, and completing missions will earn Disciple points. With enough points, they can exchange them for various rewards at the mission hall."

"The ultimate reward for exchange is to be urged by This Sect Master for one day!"

"For the specific details, you Elders should discuss them and present the final result to me."

Red Feather and Up and Down's expressions immediately became serious, "Yes! Sect Master!"

After arranging all this, Chu Xian finally walked back to his recliner with satisfaction.

Before long, Lingxiao Sect was once again in turmoil!

Red Feather and Up and Down worked together, directly causing a mission hall to rise from the ground next to the Sect Square!

Numerous facilities inside also continuously flew into the mission hall at the hands of the two Great Emperors!

Many Disciples heard the commotion and rushed over to watch.



50: The Mission Hall is now open! The Lingxiao Sect is recruiting disciples on a large scale!

As soon as the Lingxiao Sect Mission Hall appeared, a large number of Disciples had already arrived at the scene.

Before long, the Mission Hall was already built.

Then Red Feather's voice echoed throughout the entire Lingxiao Sect:

"Attention, all Disciples, a notice will now be broadcast!"

"The Sect's Mission Hall has opened."

"The Mission Hall has four floors, corresponding to Heaven, Earth, Profound, and Yellow Rank missions respectively. Heaven-Grade missions are the most difficult, and Yellow-Grade missions are the easiest."

"Missions of different Ranks require Disciples of different cultivation levels to complete."

"Completing missions in the Mission Hall will not only earn mission rewards but also a certain amount of mission points."

"Mission points can be exchanged for various Cultivation resources."

"For details on point exchange, please enter the Mission Hall and check for yourselves."

"The ultimate reward for point exchange: you can go to the Sect's Main Peak to receive a full day of personal encouragement from Sect Master!"

"Please Cultivate diligently, actively complete missions, and aim to be encouraged by the Sect Master!"

As soon as this news came out, the entire Lingxiao Sect instantly entered a state of excitement!

"Sect Master's encouragement?! My heavens! I heard that all Inner Sect Disciple's cultivation levels surged because of the Sect Master's personal encouragement!"

"Yes, yes, yes! I heard that too! With the Sect Master's little whip, cultivation levels rise like crazy!"

"Is that true? Good! Brothers! Charge! Into the great hall!"

"Ollie give! Let's do it, brothers!"

In just an instant, the Mission Hall was completely packed!

Red Feather and Up and Down exchanged glances, not expecting the Mission Hall to be so popular after opening.

"It seems there aren't enough Elders in the Mission Hall!"

"Quickly report to the Sect Master!"

After Chu Xian understood the situation, he slapped his forehead!

"Oh, I forgot about the other rewards from the thousand consecutive draws!"

"You two wait a moment, I'll solve it right away!"

Immediately, Chu Xian turned his gaze back to the System panel:

"1000 consecutive draw rewards have been distributed to the System backpack."

"Great Sage Realm NPC Elder × 32, Sub-Saint Realm NPC Elder × 66, Hedao Realm NPC Elder × 108, Saint Medicine × 136 plants..."

"Use all NPC Elders!"

"206 NPC Elders have been modeled!"

The next moment, over two hundred powerful auras suppressing heaven and earth suddenly rose within the Lingxiao Sect!

"Boom boom boom!"

Over two hundred figures instantly soared into the sky above the Lingxiao Sect, flying towards the Lingxiao Sect's Main Peak!

Above Medicine Peak.

Sai Chunchun was demonstrating to Little Daidai how to precisely control the heat when his body suddenly trembled!

"Bang!"

With a loud bang, the Pill Furnace directly exploded!

"Revered Master, your fire control technique... isn't good either..."

Sai Chunchun didn't even hear what Little Daidai said, his eyes fixed on the more than 200 figures in the sky.

Old Man Sai said blankly, "Is this the edge of the Eastern Barrens?"

"Over two hundred Saint-rank Experts!"

"Chu Xian, are you trying to sweep across the entire Xuanxiao Continent?"

Above the Main Peak, 206 figures stood neatly.

Chu Xian's heart was also surging with emotion!

If these people in front of him could step out of the Lingxiao Sect's boundaries, they would simply crush the entire Xuanxiao Continent!

However, no matter how awesome the System's offerings were, there were still limitations.

So many formidable individuals could only obediently stay within the Sect to guard the home. Isn't that infuriating?

Even if you gave me ten thousand, it would be useless!

Damn! Trash System!

Long Aotian, in the back mountain of the Sect, opened his two Dragon eyes, glanced in the direction of the Main Peak, and then obediently closed his eyes again:

Do you see? This is Revered Master's connections!

All newly joined Elders!

Over two hundred Saints!

Learn, brother!

With the addition of these more than two hundred Saints, the number of Elders in the Lingxiao Sect is now completely sufficient.

The current number of Lingxiao Sect Disciples is a little over one thousand, and the number of NPC Elders, including the two Emperor Realm experts, totals 228 people.

Even after deducting the fixed Elders required to guard various Sect facilities, the number of Elders is still very large.

On average, one NPC can teach about five Disciples.

In the period that followed, dao boats continuously arrived within the Lingxiao Sect.

Each dao boat carried exactly one hundred Disciples, all of them Genius elites and Children of Luck from various counties of the Great Xia Empire!

In addition to the dao boats bringing Disciples in batches, a large number of Cultivators who wanted to join the Lingxiao Sect also appeared outside the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate.

Most of these Cultivators came from the eastern counties of the Great Xia Empire.

To save on the Spirit Stone consumption generated by dao boat operations, the Imperial Family simply allowed several counties closer to the Lingxiao Sect to travel to the Lingxiao Sect themselves to participate in the entrance trial.

And the name of the Lingxiao Sect was also rapidly sweeping across the entire Great Xia Empire!

On this day.

Nearly a million people had gathered outside the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate!

Almost all of the younger generation from several counties in eastern Great Xia Empire had come to the entrance of the Lingxiao Sect!

This was simply because the Lingxiao Sect's entrance trial was too peculiar; as long as one obtained the qualification to enter, they could join the Lingxiao Sect.

After hearing Red Feather's report, Chu Xian waved his hand and loudly proclaimed:

"Open the Sect and accept Disciples!"

Subsequently, the entire Lingxiao Sect, from top to bottom, began to operate.

Missions were frantically issued in the Mission Hall, with the content:

Assist the Sect in recruiting Disciples, one Mortal-Grade Spirit Stone per day!

Mission point reward: 10 points.

When all the Disciples heard that the mission points were actually 10 points, they became like wolves and tigers!

This was because mission points were extremely difficult to obtain; even Heaven-Grade missions only yielded 5 points, and it took 100 points to be encouraged by the Sect Master once. Anyone who didn't participate in this mission was simply an idiot!

Thus, all Disciples of the Lingxiao Sect went out to greet guests!

As soon as over a thousand Disciples appeared, they instantly took complete control of the scene!

Yu Jian, as the Direct Disciples Senior Brother, took the lead, standing with his hands behind his back above the Mountain Gate. After explaining the Sect's trial rules, he leaped down.

This scene utterly captivated the female Cultivators who were about to participate in the entrance trial.

"Wow! This is the Direct Disciple of Lingxiao Holy Land! So handsome!"

"Oh, if only I could become Yu Jian Senior Brother's Dao Companion, that would be wonderful!"

"..."

Hearing these words, some male Cultivators immediately became displeased!

"You all? Stop dreaming! Yu Jian Senior Brother's Dao Companion is the Imperial Family's Princess!"

Instantly, countless female Cultivators clutched their chests, beating their breasts and stamping their feet... While the Disciple recruitment trial outside the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate was in full swing, a figure suddenly appeared in the sky above the western border of the Great Xia Empire.

This person stood in the air, treading on a long sword, his brows tightly furrowed.

"I didn't expect the Demonic Cult here to be so rampant! Pity these countless Mortals!"

"The coordinates Old Man Sai gave me are not far from here. I'll find that old fellow first!"

This person was none other than Duan Hong, the number one Blacksmith in the Eastern Barrens!

Duan Hong infused a trace of spiritual power into the long sword beneath his feet, and just as he was about to shoot away, he didn't expect a black-robed figure to fly up from the ground!

The newcomer was clearly also a Saint-rank Expert, and without a word, he raised his hand to attack!

Duan Hong was stunned!

"Audacious Demonic Cultivator!"

Duan Hong roared, and the long sword beneath his feet was directly grasped in his hand.

"Slash!"

The long sword instantly slashed out!

The Demonic Cultivator was instantly cut into two by the long sword in Duan Hong's hand!

Clearly, the long sword in Duan Hong's hand was both a flying Magical Artifact and a powerful weapon!

After slaying the Saint-Rank Demonic Cultivator with one sword, Duan Hong then stepped on the long sword and shot eastward.

In just half an incense stick's time, Duan Hong had already arrived outside the Lingxiao Sect's Mountain Gate!

Half an incense stick, directly traversing the entire Great Xia Empire!

Even a Great Sage Realm expert relying on his own flight would take at least ten days!
